# Findings

Every entry MUST carry `- regime:` and `- source:` lines; `make lint` enforces
this (see DECISIONS D-4). A finding cited outside its regime is a bug.

Reading this file (index header added 2026-09-05; every entry below is unchanged):

- Entries are registered text. Once an entry lands it is never rewritten, and
  numbers inside it stand as registered even where a later reading is better.
- Corrections and later readings live INSIDE the entry as dated addenda,
  notes, or errata. A dated note supersedes only the sentence it names; the
  original wording stays in place next to it (F-18 and F-22 carry theirs
  in-entry; F-16 and F-19 point to dated errata in the docs/BLUEPRINT.md
  blocks they name).
- Every entry carries `- date:`, `- regime:` and `- source:` lines. The
  regime + source pair is enforced by tools/lint_findings.py (D-4), which
  parses every `## F-` heading and checks that cited paths under results/,
  experiments/, tests/, docs/, src/, data/ and tools/ exist and (since
  2026-10-01) are tracked by git; brace shorthand and a bare file name after
  a cited path resolve too. Do not add a `## F-` heading
  for anything that is not a new finding.
- Three layers are never conflated: existence (K-2; probe margins at offsets
  >= 2), conversion (K-3; emitted tokens per verify call at matched drafting
  budget) and reconstruction (OQ-16 Stage R; linear recovery of the anchor
  hidden state). No wall-clock or speedup claim exists anywhere (D-9).
- Status words in the table below: stands (verdict unchanged), qualified (a
  later finding or a dated in-entry note narrows scope or meaning),
  superseded-in-part (a named sentence is superseded by a dated note inside
  the same entry). They are defined in docs/FINDINGS_INDEX.md.
- Map of the findings by arc, with headline numbers, CIs and dependencies:
  docs/FINDINGS_INDEX.md. House terms: docs/GLOSSARY.md. Plan of record:
  docs/BLUEPRINT.md. Kill criteria: docs/WHY_THIS_DIES.md.
- The table is a derived reading aid; on any disagreement the entry wins.

## F-0 — Verifier is exact; steering is lossless by construction
- date: 2026-08-28
- regime: mathematical property of the Leviathan rejection scheme; holds for any p, q on a shared vocab, including arbitrarily steered q
- source: tests/test_specdec.py, src/sideband/specdec.py
- finding: closed-form induced emission distribution equals the target exactly (enumeration, atol 1e-12, 200 random pairs + adversarial edge cases); property survives arbitrary bucket-bias steering of q. Steering is a pure speed knob in this scheme; output quality is not on the table.
- caveats: exactness is per-position given the same target distribution; the real-model adapter must therefore feed the SAME temperature/processing into p on both paths (hf_specdec does).

## F-1 — Exactness holds at machine epsilon under measurement
- date: 2026-08-28
- regime: mathematical property; 2000 random (p, q) pairs, vocab 2–64, sparsity-injected q, steered variants with beta in [-6, 9]
- source: results/e01_verifier_exactness.json, experiments/e01_verifier_exactness.py
- finding: worst closed-form deviation |induced - p| = 2.2e-16 (float64 epsilon) with and without steering; empirical max deviation 3.2e-4 over 500k samples (within sampling noise, sigma ~= 7e-4). The floor the project stands on is solid.

## F-2 — Most rejections are cross-bucket, and more so as the draft degrades
- date: 2026-08-28
- regime: synthetic concept-HMM v0, default WorldConfig except draft_alpha; state-observed target; buckets = ground-truth blocks; gamma=4; 24k positions x 3 seeds
- source: results/e02_crossbucket_audit.json, experiments/e02_crossbucket_audit.py
- finding: cross-bucket fraction of rejections = 89.3% (draft_alpha=0.5), 83.1% (0.7), 76.1% (0.9); vanilla acceptance 0.546 / 0.618 / 0.694. In this regime the large majority of failures are in-principle addressable by concept steering, and the addressable share grows exactly where speedup headroom is largest (weak drafts).
- caveats: this bounds the SHARE of rejections addressable, not acceptance itself — within-bucket token choice still has to match. Real-model fraction is OQ-2 and is the first GPU task.

## F-3 — Uniform bucket boosting nearly self-cancels; disagreement gating fixes it
- date: 2026-08-28
- regime: synthetic concept-HMM v0, default WorldConfig; gamma=4; 16k positions; oracle policies Dated clause 2026-10-16: steering mechanism argmax, run_simulation's parameter default; the script passes no steer_mech, so D-10's posterior default did not apply (numbers unaffected; DECISIONS.md D-10, dated note).
- source: results/e03_oracle_ceiling.json, experiments/e03_oracle_ceiling.py
- finding: always-on boosting peaks at a trivial +0.010 acceptance (stale oracle 0.6294 vs vanilla 0.6194 at beta=0.5) and collapses to 0.4376 at beta=6 (-0.18): wrong-concept positions gain, already-right positions pay the function-word tax, and the two nearly cancel. Gating the boost on disagreement (draft's own top CONCEPT bucket != predicted bucket) keeps the gains and skips the tax: stale oracle 0.6389 at beta=3 (+0.0195, ~2x the ungated peak), fresh oracle 0.6653 at beta=4 (+0.046), while steering only ~1/3 of positions. Best-beta moves from 0.5 (ungated) to 3-4 (gated) — gating buys headroom to push harder where it matters.
- caveats: the gate comparison MUST live in concept space with the function bucket excluded on both sides. With lambda_function=0.45 and a temperature-flattened draft, the draft's raw bucket-mass argmax is the FUNCTION bucket at every position, which made every position look like a disagreement and reproduced the ungated tax (bug found and fixed during build; see D-7). Real-vocab analog (glue-bucket exclusion) is OQ-4.

## F-4 — Staleness tax: held-bucket steering pays only when persistence beats gamma
- date: 2026-08-28
- regime: synthetic concept-HMM v0, default WorldConfig except rho; disagreement-gated beta=2.5; gamma=6; 14k positions; analytic per-position acceptance (unconditional, D-9) Dated clause 2026-10-16: steering mechanism argmax, run_simulation's parameter default; the script passes no steer_mech, so D-10's posterior default did not apply (numbers unaffected; DECISIONS.md D-10, dated note); one seed (200) per curve.
- source: results/e04_staleness_decay.json, experiments/e04_staleness_decay.py
- finding: stale-minus-vanilla acceptance at offset 1 -> 6: rho=0.6 (run length 2.5): -0.004 -> -0.026 (net harmful at every offset); rho=0.75: +0.004 -> -0.019; rho=0.90: +0.013 -> -0.004; rho=0.97 (run length 33): +0.018 -> +0.019 (no visible decay). The fresh oracle is flat across offsets at every rho, so the decay is purely informational staleness, not the mechanism. Rule of thumb: one telemetry read per block is only worth holding when expected concept run length comfortably exceeds gamma; otherwise gate harder or steer fewer offsets.
- caveats: draft concept corruption is i.i.d. per position here; real drafts drift cumulatively within a block, which should make deep offsets MORE steerable than this world suggests (favorable), while real concept persistence is unknown (OQ-3).
DATED ERRATUM 2026-10-16 (image audit 2026-10-15, section B item 5; docs/audits/2026-10-15-image-audit/f4_seeds.py reruns e04's protocol at seeds 200 to 209, the seed-200 run reproducing the committed payload to 1e-12): "(net harmful at every offset)" at rho=0.6 is not supported at offset 1. The stale-minus-vanilla analytic acceptance at offset 1 reads -0.0039, +0.0001, -0.0015, -0.0012, -0.0003, -0.0022, +0.0047, +0.0010, +0.0029, +0.0057 across the ten seeds, mean +0.0005 (SE 0.0010), negative on 5 of 10, and the committed seed-200 value is the most negative of the ten. Offsets 2 to 6 are negative on 10 of 10 (means -0.0121 / -0.0194 / -0.0228 / -0.0259 / -0.0267), and offset 1 at rho 0.75 and 0.9 is positive on 10 of 10 (means +0.0097 / +0.0174), so the decay curve and the rule of thumb stand; the offset-1 sign at rho 0.6 is seed noise. Every e04 curve is one seed (200); the mechanism is argmax (the dated clause on the regime line).

## F-5 — Sideband width: bad decoders are harmful ungated, harmless gated
- date: 2026-08-28
- regime: synthetic concept-HMM v0, hardened telemetry (support_gain=0.5, support_noise=0.5, background_noise=0.25, top_n=16), n_slots swept; disagreement-gated beta=2.5; gamma=4; 12k positions; sidecar = softmax regression on slot multi-hot Dated clause 2026-10-16: steering mechanism argmax, run_simulation's parameter default; the script passes no steer_mech, so D-10's posterior default did not apply (numbers unaffected; DECISIONS.md D-10, dated note).
- source: results/e05_sidecar_recovery.json, experiments/e05_sidecar_recovery.py
- finding: decoder holdout accuracy vs K slots: 0.16 (K=8), 0.38, 0.63, 0.83, 0.98, 1.00 (K=1024). Recovery of the stale-oracle gain, ungated (tau=0): -152%, -88%, -22%, +36%, +91%, +95% — a sub-80%-accuracy decoder is WORSE than no steering. With confidence gating tau=0.5: -1%, +2%, +8%, +54%, +86%, +99% — harmful regimes become harmless, the mid-K regime improves outright, and the only cost is ~6 points at K=256 where the decoder was already excellent. Design rule: the confidence gate is not optional; tau trades a small tax at high decoder quality for safety everywhere else.
- caveats: recovery is measured against the stale oracle (the causal ceiling), in a world where that ceiling itself is modest (+0.016). Absolute numbers are regime-bound; the SHAPE (gating rescues narrow sidebands) is the transferable claim.

## F-6 — This world provably cannot show telemetry beating free information
- date: 2026-08-28
- regime: synthetic concept-HMM v0 — structural property of the world, not a tuning outcome. Dated clause 2026-10-16: the e03 numbers quoted here ran at the argmax mechanism, run_simulation's parameter default (DECISIONS.md D-10, dated note).
- source: results/e03_oracle_ceiling.json, tests/test_steering_pipeline.py
- finding: free_lastp (steer from the target's last verified distribution — information every spec decoder gets for free) and oracle_concept_stale produce IDENTICAL curves (both best 0.6389 at beta=3, equal at every beta and gate). By construction both are deterministic functions of the same latent concept at the same position, so a telemetry sidecar can at best MATCH the free baseline here. Pinned as a test so nobody ever cites a synthetic "telemetry win."
- caveats: this is the reason the project's existence question (OQ-1) can only be answered on real models: does the address stream carry predictive information about offsets >= 2 BEYOND the last verified p? SD^2's hidden-state result says the information exists in the residual stream; whether it survives hashing to an address set is the bet.

## F-7 — The posterior mechanism dominates argmax steering at every width
- date: 2026-08-28
- regime: part A: synthetic concept-HMM v0, default WorldConfig, n=12000, seeds 400-401, beta=2.5, disagreement gate; part B: hardened telemetry (support_gain=0.5, support_noise=0.5, background_noise=0.25, top_n=16), n=8000, seeds 401-402
- source: results/e06_posterior_mechanism.json, experiments/e06_posterior_mechanism.py, tests/test_posterior_mechanism.py
- finding: replacing the argmax single-bucket boost with a CENTERED posterior-mass bias (beta*(P(b)-1/C) per concept bucket, glue 0) improves every policy on the default world — stale oracle +0.0165 vs +0.0124, fresh oracle +0.0272 vs +0.0255, free_lastp +0.0152 vs +0.0124 over vanilla 0.6197. On hardened worlds the posterior sidecar's recovery vs the ARGMAX oracle is +0.17 / +0.78 / +1.11 at K=16/64/256, against -0.02 / +0.50 / +0.96 for the best argmax variant; vs the mechanism-matched POSTERIOR oracle it is +0.15 / +0.67 / +0.95. The posterior oracle itself beats the argmax oracle (0.6365 vs 0.6343) on identical information and gating: the optimal bias is BIDIRECTIONAL — boost the predicted bucket AND demote the other topical buckets while sparing glue. Confidence-tau is subsumed by centering (a mushy posterior is a near-no-op); the disagreement gate remains load-bearing (ungated, a confident posterior applies near-full bias at already-right positions — the F-3 tax; reproduce with steer_gate="always").
- caveats: recovery exceeding 1.0 against the argmax oracle is a statement about the WRONG CEILING, not the decoder (D-11). Free-baseline hygiene under this mechanism: the soft bucket-mass free variant (+0.0152) sits below the one-hot stale oracle because posterior SHAPE now matters; the fair free baseline includes the SHARPENED variant (one-hot at argmax p_last), which in this world coincides exactly with the stale oracle — F-6 stands; on real models run BOTH free variants.

## F-8 — Sampled and analytic cross-bucket estimators agree; mass is now the default
- date: 2026-08-28
- regime: synthetic concept-HMM v0, default WorldConfig except draft_alpha; gamma=4; 24k positions x 3 seeds (same runs as F-2, reproduced bit-exact after the v0.2.0 pipeline changes)
- source: results/e02_crossbucket_audit.json, src/sideband/pipeline.py, src/sideband/real/hf_specdec.py
- finding: the analytic estimator (mean residual-distribution mass outside the proposed token's bucket) tracks the sampled fraction within sampling noise: 0.8924 vs 0.8933 (alpha=0.5), 0.8358 vs 0.8315 (0.7), 0.7624 vs 0.7611 (0.9). Same estimand, zero sampling variance, and it removes the "your estimator is biased toward what q underweights" review objection before it's raised. The real adapter now logs cross_bucket_residual_mass per rejection from the full p it already holds.
- caveats: v0.1 numbers (F-2) reproduced exactly under the rewritten pipeline (e02 alpha=0.7 acceptance 0.6182, sampled 0.8315; e03 vanilla 0.6194399309459379 bit-exact) — the mechanism additions consumed no extra RNG, so paired comparisons across versions remain valid.

## F-9 — Hint-rate ledger v0: dense per-position hints lose 20:1; free steering pays outright
- date: 2026-08-28
- regime: synthetic concept-HMM v0, default WorldConfig; posterior mechanism, disagreement gate, beta=2.5, gamma=4, n=16000, seeds 500-501; ideal-coding ledger, accept/reject indicator stream omitted, H1 hint priced per drafted position (conservative)
- source: results/e07_hint_rate.json, experiments/e07_hint_rate.py
- finding: vanilla pays 1.270 correction bits per emitted token (0.350 rejections/token). Free-causal steering (zero hint bits) cuts that by 0.100 bits/token — the verified-archives track's first move costs nothing. A dense per-position fresh-concept hint (log2 12 = 3.58 bits per drafted position = 3.34 bits/token after re-drafts) buys only 0.162 bits/token of corrections back: net -3.18 bits/token, a 20:1 loss even against its own ceiling. The TOTAL break-even hint budget in this regime is 0.162 bits/token — so archive hint channels must be event-driven or sub-bit (topic-shift flags, rejection-anticipating hints, block-sparse codes), never dense per-position state.
- caveats: v0 ledger only (no indicator-stream cost, ideal coding assumed, most expensive hint scheme priced); the budget number is regime-bound but the SHAPE (dense hints are dominated; free steering is a pure win) is the transferable constraint for OQ-7. Real-text ledger on the Llama pair is the follow-up.
DATED ERRATUM 2026-10-16 (image audit 2026-10-15, section B item 5; docs/audits/2026-10-15-image-audit/e07_ledger.py reruns e07's three arms with the bonus token priced, the correction bits reproducing the committed payload to four places): the v0 ledger omits the bonus token the verifier emits when a whole block is accepted, a term that is not arm-invariant: 0.0628 / 0.0654 / 0.0684 bonus tokens per emitted token on vanilla / free-causal / fresh-hint at 5.54 / 5.52 / 5.57 bits each, 0.348 / 0.361 / 0.381 unpriced bits per emitted token. With it priced the savings read 0.0862 (free-causal) and 0.1291 (fresh hint) bits per emitted token against the committed 0.0997 and 0.1621, so the committed figures are 16% and 26% high and the TOTAL break-even budget of 0.162 bits/token is overstated by about a quarter; the dense hint's loss reads about 26:1 on the fuller ledger (20:1 as committed). Direction and shape stand; the accept/reject indicator stream stays unpriced as caveated (its sign depends on the coding convention).

## F-10 — Real rejections are overwhelmingly cross-bucket; kill criterion K-1 does not fire
- date: 2026-08-29
- regime: REAL MODELS, first GPU result (Phase 0a) — Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct target in NF4 (double-quant, bf16 compute; --load-4bit) + Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct draft in bf16, one shared tokenizer (enforced); gamma=4, temperature 1.0, chat template applied with pinned date; 210 committed short prompts (data/prompts.jsonl, unique topics); seed 0 with per-prompt RNG streams; KV-cached path — pre-registered cached-vs-uncached A/B PASSED bidirectionally on all six containments (tools/verify_phase0a.py); buckets = k-means over the target's lm_head rows (PCA-128, seed 0), k in {256, 512, 1024}
- source: results/real/audit_k256.summary.json, results/real/audit_k512.summary.json, results/real/audit_k1024.summary.json, results/real/sanity_k512.summary.json, results/real/sanity_k512_nokv.summary.json, results/real/sanity_k512_bf16.summary.json, results/real/phase0a_verification.json, tools/verify_phase0a.py, src/sideband/real/hf_specdec.py, docs/WHY_THIS_DIES.md
- finding: mean acceptance 0.763 over 210 prompts, inside the ~0.6-0.8 sanity band pre-registered in the port checklist (docs/BLUEPRINT.md); NF4-vs-bf16 prompt-paired acceptance delta -0.002 pooled (+0.001 mean-of-rates), CI95 [-0.021, +0.018] over 20 prompts (pinned recipe in tools/verify_phase0a.py). Of 11,287 rejections, cross-bucket RESIDUAL MASS (the F-8 default estimand) = 0.778 [0.769, 0.787] at k=256, 0.812 [0.804, 0.820] at k=512, 0.863 [0.856, 0.870] at k=1024 (prompt-cluster bootstrap 95% CIs; the sampled fraction agrees within 0.003 at every k — F-8's estimator concordance replicates on real models). The pre-registered kill rule (mass CI upper bound < 0.25 at every k) DOES NOT FIRE; no CI intersects the [0.15, 0.35] caution band, so no verdict rides on the quantization choice. The SAMPLED FRACTION by offset (survival-conditioned per D-9) is flat across 1-4 (k=512: 0.815/0.818/0.809/0.807, n=4321/3015/2310/1641). The three k rows re-bucketize identical trajectories — identical by construction (at steer=none, buckets touch only audit labels) and empirically confirmed at every logged coordinate: all 11,287 rejection tuples and all 210 per-prompt block/accept counts are equal across k (accepted-token identity between rejections is implied by construction, not logged) — so the k-trend, finer buckets more cross-bucket, is exactly paired. Bucket-level disagreement is the dominant failure mode of this pair, where the synthetic world said it should be (F-2 synthetic: 76-89%; real: 78-86%).
- caveats: NF4-quantized target is the primary regime; the 20-prompt bf16 confirmation arm reaches the same conclusion (mass 0.787 [0.764, 0.809]), with a real-but-barely-resolved quantization shift on mass: +0.031 pooled, paired prompt-bootstrap CI95 [+0.003, +0.060] excluding zero (+0.033 in the per-prompt-mean frame) — carry it when comparing across load modes. "Cross-bucket" is BUCKET-level, not certified concept-level: glue-involved crossings are not yet excluded (OQ-4); quantify the glue share from the committed records before Phase 0b interprets the headroom. Audit metas record git_commit -dirty (then-uncommitted evidence files present during the runs); behavioral equivalence with the clean-tree sanity segment verified bit-exact on the 20-prompt overlap (tools/verify_phase0a.py check D). Short prompts probe short-prefix behavior only. One seed (CIs are cross-prompt, not cross-seed). This bounds the SHARE of rejections that concept steering can address, not the acceptance gain itself — F-2's caveat applies verbatim; Phase 0b (free-information steering) is the next bar.

## F-11 — Real glue buckets are concentrated and identifiable; the concept-level share survives (OQ-4 answered; F-10 reopen trigger does not fire)
- date: 2026-08-30
- regime: REAL MODELS (Phase 0a evidence re-analyzed + Phase 0b vanilla arm) — same pair/regime as F-10 (NF4 8B target, bf16 1B draft, gamma=4, temp 1.0, 210 committed prompts, seed 0); glue identified by the K-7 pre-registered rule (top-100 tokens by frequency in a 53,441-token TARGET-ONLY sampled harvest on the same prompts, greedy 80% frequency-mass cover, rule fixed before any glue set existed); mass-level decomposition from the Phase 0b vanilla re-run at k=512, which reproduced the committed audit trajectories BIT-IDENTICALLY (all 210 per-prompt counts, all 11,287 rejection tuples, all float64 masses — run-to-run CUDA determinism on this box/version stack, so the decomposition applies exactly to the F-10 trajectories)
- source: results/real/gen_freq.json, results/real/glue_k256.json, results/real/glue_k512.json, results/real/glue_k1024.json, results/real/glue_phase0a_analysis.json, results/real/phase0b_vanilla_k512.summary.json, tools/analyze_glue.py, src/sideband/glue.py, docs/WHY_THIS_DIES.md
- finding: glue is CONCENTRATED, exactly as the D-7 analogy requires — 2 buckets at k=256 (of 256), 2 at k=512, 4 at k=1024 cover 80% of top-100 frequency mass (82.3%/80.1%/80.0%; concentration flag fires nowhere); decoded members are textbook function tokens (", the, ., a, and, of, to, 's", markdown markers, pronouns). Excluding them: concept-to-concept rejections (emitted token in a non-glue bucket different from the proposed token's bucket) = 0.565 [0.554, 0.575] at k=256, 0.610 [0.600, 0.621] at k=512, 0.674 [0.664, 0.684] at k=1024 (prompt-cluster bootstrap CIs); the mass-level analog at k=512 (residual mass in other-concept buckets, from the vanilla arm's per-record decomposition) = 0.607 [0.597, 0.617] — sampled and analytic estimators concordant at concept level too. The pre-registered F-10 REOPEN TRIGGER (concept-level CI upper < 0.25, token-level at every k or mass-level at k=512) DOES NOT FIRE; no CI touches the [0.15, 0.35] caution band. Glue involvement is substantial (39-49% of rejections have proposed or emitted in glue; ~32% on each side at k=512, ~35% at k=256; glue tokens are 41% of generated text) — the honest haircut from F-10's bucket-level 0.78-0.86 to concept-level 0.56-0.67 — but the majority of real rejections are genuine concept jumps. Recomputed bucket-level summaries match the committed F-10 summaries bit-exactly (internal consistency PASS).
- caveats: "concept-level" means non-glue k-means buckets, not certified semantic concepts. The rule's 80% cover is a pre-registered but arbitrary threshold; the committed sensitivity RE-ANALYSIS (alternative_glue_sets in results/real/glue_phase0a_analysis.json) measures the estimand under every committed alternative set: 90%-cover glue gives concept-cross 0.496/0.545/0.583 by k, the count-rule (>=5 of top-100) sets give 0.496/0.539/0.624 — every alternative's CI lower bound >= 0.485, ~2x the 0.25 bar, so the trigger verdict is insensitive to the rule choice. Frequency source is NF4 seed-0 generations on the same short-prompt set (regime-bound; a different prompt mix shifts the top-100 modestly). One seed.

## F-12 — Free-information steering baseline is ZERO on real models; the F-3 gate tax reproduces, diagnosed as gate-granularity failure (Phase 0b)
- date: 2026-08-30
- regime: REAL MODELS (Phase 0b) — same pair/regime as F-10/F-11 (NF4 8B + bf16 1B, gamma=4, temp 1.0, 210 prompts, seed 0, KV-cached, chat template pinned); k=512 buckets with glue {59, 403} excluded per F-11; steer=bucket, mech=posterior (D-10), gate=disagree; BOTH D-11 free variants (soft = concept-bucket masses of last verified p, sharp = one-hot at its argmax) x beta {1,2,3,4}, plus the pre-registered always-gate control (sharp beta=3); paired per-prompt deltas vs the vanilla re-run at identical seeds (D-6), plug-in pooled estimates with prompt-bootstrap CI95 (seed 0, n_boot 20000); confirmatory arm named in advance: sharp beta=3. All 12 arms (11 sweep + the cover90 re-test) ran code commit 6a468f1 (vanilla clean-tree; later arms' -dirty flags are results artifacts, and the cover90 arm's additionally uncommitted docs/tools edits — src/ and tests/ clean throughout, verified by git status before commit)
- source: results/real/phase0b_analysis.json, results/real/phase0b_vanilla_k512.summary.json, results/real/phase0b_sharp_b3.summary.json, results/real/phase0b_sharp_b3_always.summary.json, results/real/phase0b_sharp_b3_nokv.summary.json, results/real/phase0b_sharp_b3_cover90.summary.json, results/real/glue_k512_cover90.json, results/real/bucket_persistence_k512.json, tools/analyze_phase0b.py, tools/bucket_persistence.py, docs/BLUEPRINT.md, docs/WHY_THIS_DIES.md
- finding: the D-5 denominator is ZERO — no arm beats vanilla (acceptance 0.7628 pooled): best delta +0.0025 [-0.0028, +0.0079] (soft beta=1), CI through zero; the pre-registered CONFIRMATORY arm (sharp beta=3) is definitively NEGATIVE, -0.0098 [-0.0155, -0.0039]; harm is dose-monotone in beta for sharp (+0.0009 / -0.0040 / -0.0098 / -0.0237 [-0.0302, -0.0172]) and null-to-slightly-negative for soft (+0.0025 / +0.0002 / -0.0051 / -0.0033). The D-9 analytic estimand (recorded at every drafted position) agrees with empirical acceptance in sign and size at every arm with tighter CIs — the tax is in E[1-TV], not sampling noise. MECHANISM DIAGNOSIS, the load-bearing part: the disagreement gate is open 93.6-94.0% of drafted positions in every gated arm — the pre-registered K-7 gate-health diagnostic (>~0.8) FIRES. Argmax-vs-argmax agreement between the draft's and last-p's concept profiles is RARE over C=510 fine buckets — the observed ~6% agreement is ~30x uniform chance yet still leaves the gate effectively ungated (the synthetic gate operated over C=12). The staleness half of the failure is MEASURED, not inferred: consecutive-token concept-bucket persistence at k=512 is 0.027 (n=17,218 concept-concept pairs; mean run length 1.015, p90=1 — results/real/bucket_persistence_k512.json), so a block-held fine-bucket prediction violates F-4's hold rule by construction, whatever its source. The always-gate control completes the F-3 decomposition on real models: ungated -0.0168 [-0.0232, -0.0104] vs gated -0.0098 — the gate removes ~40% of the harm (ordering always < gated < vanilla, as F-3 predicts) but cannot rescue a mechanism that mostly bets on the wrong fine-grained bucket; unlike the synthetic world, gated steering stays net-negative. The K-7 addendum's pre-registered 90%-cover re-test was RUN before this diagnosis: tripling the glue exclusion (2 -> 6 buckets, 80% -> 91% cover) moves the gate-open rate only 0.9393 -> 0.9361 and leaves the delta <= 0 (-0.0051 [-0.0108, +0.0007]; analytic -0.0078 [-0.0125, -0.0032]) — the open rate is a granularity property, not an under-exclusion artifact. Tokens/target-call tracks acceptance (sharp beta=4: -0.146). Riders: vanilla re-run acceptance point inside audit_k512's CI (trivially - trajectories bit-identical, F-11); KV spot rider on the confirmatory arm PASSED all six bidirectional containments (first 20 prompts vs --no-kv-cache re-run).
- caveats: this bounds THE MECHANISM AT THIS GRANULARITY, not free information's ceiling — the k=512 bucket space that made the F-10/F-11 headroom look large is the same space that makes the gate uninformative and the one-hot bet mostly wrong; a coarse gating space (C ~ 12-64) with fine auditing is untested (OQ-12) and must be evaluated before concluding free information itself is worthless. Per-block-held prediction (no per-offset decay); one seed; NF4; short prompts. The by-offset analytic table is flat (vanilla 0.749/0.748/0.744/0.746), so staleness within gamma=4 blocks is not the limiting factor here. A null baseline sets K-2's bar for telemetry at "beat zero"; it BLOCKS PHASE 4 ONLY (steering through a net-harmful mechanism would fire K-3 spuriously) — Phases 2/3 proceed, since the harvest is granularity-agnostic and K-2's probes compare information content, not steering deltas (OQ-12 informs their label space). Note F-12's null also removes K-2's pre-registered salvage ("fold to free-information steering"), which makes running the existence test SOONER the right risk posture, not later.

## F-13 — Coarse gating fixes the gate, not the mechanism: OQ-12 answered NEGATIVE; free-information bucket steering is dead at every tested granularity
- date: 2026-08-30
- regime: REAL MODELS (Phase 0c stage 1) — same pair/regime as F-12 (NF4 8B + bf16 1B, gamma=4, temp 1.0, 210 committed prompts, seed 0, KV-cached); COARSE gating spaces k in {16, 32, 64} (fresh bucketize_vocab, same PCA/seed; glue by the K-7 rule verbatim: 1/1/2 buckets), all three pre-declared VIABLE (concept-cross CI lower 0.303/0.410/0.522 >= 0.25 on committed records, results/real/oq12_diagnostics.json); arms = {soft, sharp} x beta=3 per k, paired against phase0b_vanilla_k512 under the gate-space exemption (buckets/glue sha differ BY DESIGN — the treatment; soundness verified on both axes: cross-k audit identity + F-11 bit-identity); protocol, verdict cells, and criterion estimand pre-registered in commit 89173bd BEFORE any steered coarse run; all six arms ran code commit 8f2f1ba (later arms -dirty by untracked sibling results only; src/tests clean throughout)
- source: results/real/phase0c_analysis.json, results/real/phase0c_k16_soft_b3.summary.json, results/real/phase0c_k16_sharp_b3.summary.json, results/real/phase0c_k32_soft_b3.summary.json, results/real/phase0c_k32_sharp_b3.summary.json, results/real/phase0c_k64_soft_b3.summary.json, results/real/phase0c_k64_sharp_b3.summary.json, results/real/oq12_diagnostics.json, docs/BLUEPRINT.md, tools/analyze_phase0b.py
- finding: ALL SIX ARMS LAND IN THE REGISTERED BROKEN CELL — no WIN, no RESCUED-TO-NEUTRAL; OQ-12 is answered NEGATIVE by the pre-registered existential criterion, and stage 2 never triggers. The GRANULARITY DIAGNOSIS ITSELF IS CONFIRMED as a measured curve: gate-open rate is monotone in concept-space size — 0.55-0.56 at C=15, 0.67 at C=31, 0.80-0.81 at C=62, joining 0.94 at C=510 (F-12) — so coarsening genuinely restores the disagreement gate's function. It does not restore the MECHANISM: deltas vs vanilla (criterion estimand, paired pooled acceptance, plugin [CI95]) — k16 soft -0.0029 [-0.0088, +0.0029] (the only straddle; its gate 0.559 misses the registered 0.5 bar), k16 sharp -0.0124 [-0.0186, -0.0061], k32 soft -0.0126 [-0.0184, -0.0068], k32 sharp -0.0183 [-0.0246, -0.0121], k64 soft -0.0083 [-0.0141, -0.0025], k64 sharp -0.0252 [-0.0317, -0.0186]. The concordance estimand (analytic drafted, D-9) is definitively negative for ALL six including the k16-soft straddle (-0.0063 [-0.0108, -0.0016]) — the strong form (every CI upper < 0) holds on the analytic frame and fails only via k16-soft's +0.0029 acceptance upper. Sharp > soft harm at every k; sharp harm is monotone in k (-0.012/-0.018/-0.025), soft is not (-0.003/-0.013/-0.008). Mechanism reading: the failure DECOMPOSES — at fine k the gate is useless AND the held prediction stale (F-12); at coarse k the gate closes roughly half the time (SELECTIVITY restored; whether closure selects the RIGHT positions is untested — the always-gate control was stage-2-only and never triggered) yet the bias behind it still loses. The residual failure is CONSISTENT WITH the free predictor's forecast quality (concept persistence 1.07-1.14 across coarse k, 1.14 even at k=16 — the last-p bucket is a bad bet everywhere tested), but the registered overdetermination caveat STANDS: at coarse k the headroom is also halved (concept-cross 0.31 at k=16 vs 0.61 at k=512), 71% of rejections are glue-involved, one glue bucket holds 58.6% of generated mass at k=16 (the beta-renormalization tax surface is the majority of q at every steered position — a cost a PERFECT forecaster would still pay), and soft harm is non-monotone in k — single-cause attribution requires the mechanism-matched oracle-ceiling arm registered in docs/BLUEPRINT.md (Phase 0c attribution rider). Free-information bucket-bias steering (D-8/D-10 mechanism family, block-held predictions, beta=3 at coarse k) is dead AS BUILT on real models at k in {16, 32, 64, 512}, both variants, with dose-response evidence. Vanilla-rerun rider passed (point-in-CI, trivially — bit-identical trajectories); the analysis verdict string ISSUES is the registered --skip-kv-rider artifact (the 0b KV rider covers the byte-identical code path: git diff 6a468f1..8f2f1ba -- src/ is empty).
- caveats: the registered interpretation caveat applies in full — a BROKEN outcome at coarse k is OVERDETERMINED (halved headroom, glue-dominated stream, still-failing F-4 hold rule), so this finding attributes the residual failure to nothing; the oracle-ceiling arm decides. beta=3 only at coarse k (pre-registered; F-12's beta sweep at k=512 showed no beta rescues there, but a coarse-k low-beta interaction is formally untested); block-held predictions with no per-offset decay (a decayed or per-offset-gated variant is untested — though persistence ~1.1 bounds how much holding can ever be worth); the 0.5 gate bar and 0.25 viability bar are pre-registered but not derived from a cost model. This kills the free-information ACTUATOR as built, not the information question: K-2/Phase 3 compares information content directly (probes at offsets >= 2, its registered bar) and proceeds. Phase 4 remains blocked pending an actuator with a non-negative free arm (the registered condition stands). No wall-clock claims (all counts).

## F-14 — The oracle ceiling at coarse k is ~zero: perfect forecasting barely moves acceptance (<= +0.007) through the bucket-bias actuator (attribution rider: STRADDLE, with the ceiling bounded)
- date: 2026-08-30
- regime: REAL MODELS (Phase 0c attribution rider, registered with its decision rule in commit 814fc34 BEFORE the arm existed) — oracle_nextp at k=16: per drafted position the steering posterior is the concept posterior of the TARGET'S OWN fresh p_j (target stepped incrementally against its KV cache; D-2-NAMED NON-CAUSAL CEILING, bounds only); soft mech, beta=3, disagree gate, k=16 space + committed glue; same pair/prompts/seed/NF4/KV-cached as all Phase 0b/0c arms; paired vs phase0b_vanilla_k512 under the gate-space exemption; implementation gate passed before running (beta=0 oracle path reproduces the standard path bit-exactly on the tiny harness, 3 seeds x both cache modes; steered KV A/B holds; per-position-freshness pinned by spy test)
- source: results/real/phase0c_oracle_analysis.json, results/real/phase0c_k16_oracle_b3.summary.json, docs/BLUEPRINT.md, src/sideband/real/hf_specdec.py, tests/test_tools_and_real.py
- finding: REGISTERED VERDICT: STRADDLE — delta acceptance +0.0014 [-0.0043, +0.0069] (plugin, paired pooled; analytic concordance +0.0033 [-0.0010, +0.0075], positive-leaning at every offset) — attribution between forecast quality and the actuator stays formally open, reported as such per the rule. The MEASURED CEILING is the substantive result: a PERFECT per-position concept forecast, fed through the D-8/D-10 bucket-bias mechanism at the granularity where its gate demonstrably works, converts to at most +0.0069 acceptance (CI upper; point +0.0014) — the actuator's oracle ceiling at coarse k is approximately ZERO. Two distinct threads, kept apart: (1) BETA-INVARIANT — the truth-informed gate opens at only 15.9% of steerable positions (n=69,192; the gate fires BEFORE any bias, so this rate holds for any disagree-gated variant): at C=15 the draft's top concept bucket already agrees with the target's fresh concept profile ~84% of the time, so almost no concept-level disagreement remains to steer. (2) BETA-SPECIFIC — at beta=3 soft, exploiting those 16% converts to at most +0.0069. The free predictor (F-13: gate 55.9%) opens ~3.5x as often as the truth-gate (comparability footnote: the free arm's n_steerable excludes each prompt's first block, the oracle's includes it, and each rate is measured on its own steered trajectory — the ratio is approximate on both axes); the free-vs-oracle direct paired gap is +0.0043 with CI THROUGH ZERO [-0.0016, +0.0102], so the "excess opens are what the errors cost" reading is CONSISTENT with the data but NOT DECOMPOSED (separating gating errors from direction errors would need a mechanism-crossed arm that was not registered and did not run). THE GRANULARITY TRAP, at every TESTED cell: fine k has headroom (0.61 concept-cross) but a useless gate and stale held forecasts (F-12); coarse k has a working gate and fixable forecasts (this arm) but true concept-disagreement collapses to 16% and the ceiling is <= +0.007. The fine-k-oracle cell (perfect forecast x large headroom) is the OPEN REMAINDER — no sweet-spot claim extends to it.
- caveats: STRADDLE means the CI answers neither registered extreme — attribution between forecast quality and the actuator stays open, and no sentence here assigns it; what IS excluded is any oracle gain above +0.0069 at this (k=16, beta=3, soft, one seed) cell (a beta sweep of the oracle was not registered and did not run). Unlike F-13, the "byte-identical src" KV argument is NOT available here (the oracle path is new code), so THREE gates covered it: the tiny-harness beta=0 bit-equivalence, the steered tiny KV A/B, and a 20-prompt real-hardware --no-kv-cache oracle spot rider that PASSED all six bidirectional containments (phase0c_k16_oracle_b3_nokv; analysis verdict OK, no rider skip). THE UNTESTED CELL, now the sharpest remaining actuator question: the FINE-k oracle (perfect forecast x the 0.61 headroom at k=512) — if it wins big, the actuator is fine and fine-k forecast quality was the whole story; if it loses, the actuator is dead outright (K-3's ceiling measured at ~0 everywhere). Not run; requires its own registration. The oracle is non-causal (D-2): nothing here says any deployable predictor reaches this ceiling — it says the ceiling itself is low. Phase 4 remains blocked; K-2/Phase 3 (information content at offsets >= 2) proceeds unchanged, with the note that at coarse granularity even perfect information has nowhere to act through THIS mechanism.

## F-15 — K-2 survived at source S1: compute-tier weight-slot telemetry carries CI-positive future-concept information beyond the strongest free baseline at offsets 2–3, replicated on fresh harvest seeds
- date: 2026-08-31
- regime: REAL MODELS (Phase 3, source S1) — probes on the committed S1 shadow harvest (Phase 2 protocol: NF4 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, shadow taps at layers {7,15,23,31} = top-32-of-512 weight-slot occupancy per layer from the anchor forward only; block-start alignment, offset 1 = the token sampled from p_a itself, K-2 reads offsets >= 2; pinned prompt split 150/30/30). ORIGINAL grid: seeds 0..7, 1,693,036 pairs, 156 registered cells + 6 R2 shuffle, commit fba1fb3-dirty. REPLICATION (registered in BLUEPRINT with its decision PINNED to the L1/off2/linear triple BEFORE any replication artifact existed): fresh generation seeds 8..11 into the separate dir harvest_shadow_r (849,536 pairs — half volume BY REGISTERED DESIGN; the harvest tool's 1M exit-bar print and the summary's EXIT_BAR_NOT_MET verdict are the Phase-2 FULL-harvest rule, superseded by the replication registration's 4-seed pin), same 156-cell grid, commit 86136c8-dirty (both runs' -dirty = untracked results/scratch only, operator-attested; tracked src/tests clean). Riders, both runs: R1 harvest fidelity PASS bound to exact npz shas (replication: 16/16 fresh triples at rng-seed 2 first attempt, calibrated tolerances, cycle bound not stressed); R2 label-shuffle PASS at every offset (relative band; replication gaps <= 1e-4); R3 clean. Label spaces L1 concept_k16 / L2 concept_k512 / L3 concept_groups_v1 (fixed committed artifact, fit on original train split only); features (a) = ALL logged free summaries of p_a (657 dims), (b) = slots multi-hot (2048), (c) = both, (h) = h_a (4096, OQ-6 denominator); capacities linear + mlp256 per the registered economy; train seeds {0,1,2}; estimand = per-prompt paired successes summed over train seeds, then ONE prompt-cluster bootstrap CI95 over the 30 test prompts (sideband.stats.paired_cluster_delta_ci, n_boot 2000, seed 0). Archival duty discharged: second on-disk copies of both harvests sha-verified (8/8 and 4/4; operator-attested — the verification ran against the committed metas but no artifact records it).
- source: results/real/probes_s1.json, results/real/probes_s1.json.state.jsonl, results/real/probes_s1_replication.json, results/real/probes_s1_replication.json.state.jsonl, results/real/harvest_shadow_r/ (metas; npz sha-bound), results/real/harvest_shadow_r_summary.json, results/real/harvest_fidelity_rider_r.json, results/real/harvest_shadow_summary.json, results/real/harvest_fidelity_rider.json, results/real/concept_groups_v1.json, results/real/harvest_audit.json, docs/BLUEPRINT.md, tools/run_probes.py, src/sideband/probes.py, src/sideband/stats.py
- finding: TELEMETRY EARNS EXISTENCE AT S1 AND THE REGISTERED REPLICATION CONFIRMS IT — the provisional qualifier lifts. Original grid: 10/12 offset->=2 (space, capacity) triples fired all three registered conditions (CI95 lower > 0, all three train seeds positive, point >= +0.005); all 12 CI-positive. Replication on fresh seeds: the PINNED gating triple L1_concept_k16/off2/linear RE-FIRED — +0.0141 [+0.0081, +0.0203], per-seed +0.0123/+0.0144/+0.0157 — and breadth reproduced beyond the pin: ALL EIGHT offset-2/offset-3 triples re-fired (8/8 across all three spaces and both capacities), with margins agreeing across runs within 0.006 (median ~0.002: L1 off2 linear +0.0163 -> +0.0141; L1 off2 mlp256 +0.0256 -> +0.0200 the widest; L2 off2 +0.0277 -> +0.0245; L3 off2 +0.0443 -> +0.0427; the four off3 triples per run sit at +0.008..+0.012 on both) — winner's-curse shrinkage is small. OFFSET 4 IS NOT ESTABLISHED: 0/4 off4 triples re-fired (replication points +0.0036/+0.0044/+0.0050/+0.0047, the L2 miss by 2.5e-5 of the floor; originally 2/4 fired marginally) — the demonstrated horizon is 2–3 tokens. STRUCTURE, the load-bearing part, reproduced on both datasets: (i) free information about the future DIES by offset 3 — the 657-dim (a) probe lands ON the majority floor at L1 off3 (original 0.5915 = floor at all three seeds; replication 0.589737 = floor to 6 decimals at s0/s1, s2 below) — while (ii) telemetry-ALONE stays CI-positive over the full free baseline ((b)-(a) CI-positive in 11/12 offset->=2 triples original, 9/12 replication, every off2 triple positive on both runs; the three replication crossings are L1|off4|mlp256 (lo -0.0003), L3|off3 (lo -0.0001), L3|off4 (lo -0.0019)), and (iii) at free-saturated offset 1 ADDING TELEMETRY HURTS — every (c)-(a) CI-negative on both runs (original -0.0049..-0.0699, replication -0.0049..-0.0998) — the internal control that excludes index-misalignment leaks, which would inflate offset 1 and which R2's circular shuffle cannot see. Critic gates on both runs: independent recomputation of every margin/CI/verdict from the state files matched BIT-EXACT; no leak channel found. Net: the compute-tier weight-slot tap carries short-horizon (2–3 token) future-concept information that the decoder's free stream demonstrably does not — K-2's registered existence question is answered YES for S1, at +0.008..+0.044 top-1 accuracy over floors of 0.41–0.59. Existence, not headroom.
- caveats: EXISTENCE != ACTUATOR — K-3 is fully open and now load-bearing: F-12/F-13 killed free-info bucket-bias steering and F-14 bounded that actuator's coarse-k oracle ceiling at ~zero, so converting these margins to acceptance needs a different actuator (or the fine-k oracle cell resolved) before Phase 4 is attemptable. PER-SOURCE SCOPE (D-1/K-2): S1 is a value-aware COMPUTE-TIER tap; this finding says NOTHING about pure address-stream sources — S2 (KV pages) and S3 (MoE routing) have their own runs of this protocol, and the hardware story AS ORIGINALLY WRITTEN still lives or dies there. MECHANISM UNRESOLVED: genuine anticipatory feature-retrieval vs persistent topic-state that p_a's summaries fail to carry — both satisfy the registered K-2 question (information beyond free), but they price the sideband differently; the k-NN/topic control and calibration pass are queued post-hoc and labeled as such (the topic-state hypothesis was subsequently tested and rejected AT THE HISTOGRAM-FAMILY LEVEL — F-16; the embedding-similarity variant remains under F-16's richer-family residual). L3's +0.0427 reads through its recorded 455/510 mega-group lens. OQ-6 UNDEFINED at every offset >= 2 on BOTH runs (original h-a off2 +0.0009 [-0.0087, +0.0107], replication -0.0012 [-0.0112, +0.0090], both crossing; h BELOW (a) at off3/off4 on both) — a statement about the registered linear budget on 4096 raw dims, NOT an information bound on the taps (registration wording stands); payload-internal evidence says budget-not-bug (h off1 0.735/0.728-0.733 >> floor on the two runs; monotone decay; val≈test). The replication resamples generations and probe training, NOT prompts — the 30 test prompt clusters are the registered population (disclosed at registration); prompt-population generalization is untested, and the regime is short factual prompts, one model (the NF4 target alone — no draft is involved in the harvest), one tap design. Multiplicity was owned at ~26% family-wise pre-replication; the pinned single-triple replication is the insurance that was registered, and it passed with breadth (8/8 off2-off3) beyond the requirement. No wall-clock claims; no acceptance claims; per-position analytic acceptance language does not apply here (this is probe accuracy, a different layer — no F-1-style conversion is implied).

Gate D +0.0002 [-0.0012, +0.0015] / +0.0012 [-0.0003, +0.0028] with Mt +0.0034 [+0.0021, +0.0048] / +0.0033 [+0.0012, +0.0052] beside it. The registered equal-dims twin was found NON-INERT after the fact (it reads the same row 17 positions ahead, beyond the label position for about 94% of anchors; Mn +0.0033 [+0.0018, +0.0049] on the primary harvest, CI-clear, and +0.0020 [-0.0000, +0.0041] on the replication, straddling), so the label carries the corrected-twin reading: TWIN-ROBUST — D_r +0.0002 [-0.0009, +0.0014] primary (seeds +0.0013 / -0.0016 / +0.0008), +0.0013 [-0.0002, +0.0028] replication (seeds +0.0029 / +0.0009 / -0.0001); Mn_r +0.0032 [+0.0016, +0.0050] / +0.0020 [+0.0002, +0.0037]; T +0.0000 [-0.0012, +0.0013] / +0.0000 [-0.0016, +0.0019] (TWIN-INFORMATION-NOT-SHOWN). The consequences act exactly as pinned; the null reproduces at a prompt-independent twin at the gate cell. The corrected twin ran the offset-2 mlp256 gate cell only, so the offset-3 leg of this scope and the linear rows rest on the registered shift twin.

## F-16 — The tap margin is not topic-state: prefix-history features predict ~nothing, add ~nothing to free, and the telemetry margin passes the equal-dims noise control intact (post-hoc P1, both harvests)
- date: 2026-08-31
- regime: REAL MODELS (post-hoc diagnostic P1; NON-CRITERION by registration — cannot amend F-15's verdict) — protocol registered in BLUEPRINT's P1 block BEFORE any diagnostic probe ran, with the budget-dilution amendment (equal-dims (atn) noise arm + amended DEFLATES gate) registered mid-flight on critic review BEFORE any result was read. Feature set (t) = 560 decoder-free dims from tokens[row, :anchor] ONLY (strictly before the offset-1 token — the anchor information set; boundary pinned by live-verified mutant tests): normalized k512-alphabet concept histogram incl. merged class (511) + k16 full-prefix (16) + last-16/last-64 k16 windows (32) + log1p(len) (1). Grid: {L1, L2, L3} x offsets {2, 3} (the F-15-established horizon) x {t, at, abt, atn} x LINEAR x train seeds {0,1,2} = 72 cells per harvest, both harvests (primary seeds 0..7; replication 8..11 as no-gate confirm); (atn) = (a)+(t)+alignment-destroyed slots (slot vector of anchor (a+17) mod row-length — per-row slot statistics preserved, alignment destroyed, dims equal to (abt) at 3265; the registered amendment text wrote 3266/1218 with the pre-correction t=561 — correct values 3265/1217, dated erratum in the BLUEPRINT P1 OUTCOME). Same estimand/bootstrap as Phase 3; M3/M4/(t)-(b) pair new cells against the COMMITTED Phase-3 state-file cluster counts (load-time gate recomputes the committed payload margins from those cells at 1e-12 before anything trains). Runner commit 1a54fb7-dirty both runs (untracked results/scratch only, operator-attested).
- source: results/real/posthoc_topic_s1.json, results/real/posthoc_topic_s1.json.state.jsonl, results/real/posthoc_topic_s1_r.json, results/real/posthoc_topic_s1_r.json.state.jsonl, tools/run_posthoc_topic.py, src/sideband/probes.py, docs/BLUEPRINT.md, results/real/probes_s1.json.state.jsonl, results/real/probes_s1_replication.json.state.jsonl
- finding: MECHANICAL LABEL: P1-SURVIVES, on both harvests. The topic-state explanation of F-15 is REJECTED on every pinned readout. (i) (t) ALONE IS BLIND: all six prefix-topic-only cells sit on the majority floor on both harvests (max |acc - floor| = 4.3e-4 primary, 7.6e-4 replication) — the concept histogram of everything the decoder has already accepted predicts ~nothing about concepts 2-3 tokens ahead, exactly what F-12's measured concept persistence (0.027, run length ~1) implied. (ii) (t) ADDS ~NOTHING TO FREE: M3 = (at)-(a) spans -0.0008..+0.0042 primary (only L3 off2 CI-positive at +0.0042 [+0.0008, +0.0077], below the +0.005 floor), max |M3| 0.0018 replication. (iii) THE TAP MARGIN PASSES INTACT: M2 = (abt)-(at) fires in ALL SIX cells on both harvests — primary L1 +0.0177 [+0.0119, +0.0232] / +0.0102 [+0.0049, +0.0158], L2 +0.0262 [+0.0200, +0.0322] / +0.0103 [+0.0055, +0.0153], L3 +0.0406 [+0.0348, +0.0460] / +0.0096 [+0.0051, +0.0139] (off2/off3); replication +0.0064..+0.0414, all CI-positive — magnitudes indistinguishable from F-15's (c)-(a). Primary cell per-seed +0.0179/+0.0175/+0.0177. (iv) THE EQUAL-DIMS CONTROL REMOVES THE LAST CONFOUND: M2n = (abt)-(atn) (3265 vs 3265 dims, alignment the only difference) fires in all six cells on both harvests (primary +0.0101..+0.0451; replication +0.0099..+0.0480), larger than or equal to M2 within 1e-4 (equal exactly where measured dilution ~ 0: 3 of 12 cells differ by <= 1e-4 in the other direction), while the measured pure-dilution cost M2noise = (atn)-(at) is small and non-positive within noise (primary -0.0045..+3.3e-5) — dilution was depressing M2, not inflating it, so the dims-asymmetric and equal-dims arms APPROXIMATELY bracket the true tap margin (within ~1e-4, under the registered noise-arm reading; the shifted-slots arm could in principle carry aligned-equivalent signal, a channel whose bias could only make M2n an UNDERestimate — SURVIVES rides on M2 and is unaffected). Descriptive: telemetry-alone beats prefix-topic-alone everywhere ((t)-(b) = -0.0093..-0.0718 primary). (t)-ALIVENESS: floor-level (t) is not a dead feature path — the CI-positive M3 rows (L3/off2 primary +0.0042 [+0.0008, +0.0077]; L2/off2 replication [+0.0006, +0.0029]) are impossible with dead (t), and the boundary mutants were live-killed at the pre-results critic gate. NET: whatever the S1 tap carries about offsets 2-3, it is NOT recoverable from the decoder's generated-token history at the histogram level — the cheapest software substitute for the sideband fails to substitute, the anticipatory-retrieval reading strengthens, and the compute-tier sideband keeps hardware-relevant pricing.
- caveats: NON-CRITERION and post-hoc by registration: this diagnoses F-15's mechanism, it is not a new K-2 verdict. The PROMPT-TOKEN RESIDUAL stands as registered: the harvest stores generated tokens only, so (t) omits prompt tokens — survival leaves a prompt-augmented prefix control as the formally open residual (expected small once the generated prefix is long: (t) is already blind with up to 255 prefix tokens, and topic is more stationary than that — but UNQUANTIFIED, and a runtime decoder would have the prompt). "Topic" here = concept-histogram summaries at four granularities; a richer prefix representation (sequence models over the prefix, embedding-similarity/k-NN over prompts, prompt embeddings) could in principle recover more — P1 rejects the HISTOGRAM FAMILY only; the queued k-NN/embedding-similarity control (docs/ECOSYSTEM.md) is a richer family and falls under this residual, NOT under this rejection, and any richer prefix control would be a new registration. Linear probes only (registered economy); 30-cluster power; L3 mega-group lens; one model, one tap design. The (atn) shift self-maps on rows shorter than 18 (the known R2 sliver, disclosed). No wall-clock or acceptance claims; K-3 is untouched — this finding says the signal is real and non-derivable from tokens, not that it converts.
- DATED NOTE (2026-09-06; F-24): the shift-17 equal-dims twin used here as the noise control is NOT inert under a null direction — it reads a position whose forward pass has consumed the label token for about 94% of anchors and gains CI-clear over the base at the OQ-17 gate on the primary harvest (Mn +0.0033 [+0.0018, +0.0049]; replication +0.0020 [-0.0000, +0.0041], straddling); the "could only make M2n an UNDERestimate" caveat holds under SURVIVES only. The mechanism reading of this finding is moot for the L1 offsets 2-3 margin at the registered reader (a margin below the materiality floor and not beyond the equal-dims twin has no mechanism to price); the histogram-level rejection of topic-state as an explanation of the PROXY margin is untouched. Not rewritten.

## F-17 — K-2 survived at source S3: pure address-stream MoE-router telemetry carries CI-positive future-concept information beyond the strongest free baseline (offset 2 in every space; to offsets 3-4 under coarse-graining), replicated on fresh harvest seeds
- date: 2026-09-01
- regime: REAL MODELS (Phase 3, source S3) — probes on the committed S3 MoE-router harvest: allenai/OLMoE-1B-7B-0125-Instruct, NF4 double-quant with routers+lm_head excluded from quantization (llm_int8_skip_modules leaf-name matched; verify_load_regime post-load inspection writes the regime into every meta from inspection, not flags); telemetry = the model's own top-8-of-64 expert ids at each of the 16 MoE gates, anchor forward only (sorted int16, npz key "slots", 64-expert alphabet) — ADDRESSES ONLY, no router logits, no probabilities, no values; block-start alignment, offset 1 = the token sampled from p_a itself, K-2 reads offsets >= 2; pinned prompt split 150/30/30 clusters (210 prompts). PRIMARY grid: generation seeds 0..7, 1,647,264 pairs (volume bar met; splits 1,174,184/235,460/237,620), 156 registered cells + 6 R2 shuffle, partial=false, commit 808266e-dirty. Riders, both harvests: R1-S3 fidelity 16/16 PASS first attempt at rng-seed 0 / 1 (registered lineage), calibrated tolerances (expert overlap >= 5/8, mass L-inf 0.20, entropy 0.50, argmax-gap 0.20), worst observed overlap 7/8 on both, worst mass L-inf 5.57e-02 / 2.62e-02 vs calibration band 0.0666; R2 label-shuffle PASS at every offset on both runs (primary gaps <= 1.7e-5, replication <= 3.7e-4); audits clean (contamination test-in-train distinct/occurrence 0.52%/0.64% primary, 0.37%/0.47% replication; loopy rows 0 on both). Label spaces L1_olmoe_k16 / L2_olmoe_k512 (concept + merged glue class 510) / L3_olmoe_groups_v1 (fixed committed artifact, fit on the primary train chain only, sha-identical for both grids, never refit); features (a) = ALL logged free summaries of p_a, (b) = expert multi-hot (16 x 64 = 1024 dims), (c) = both, (h) = 2048-dim hidden reference (OQ-6 denominator); capacities linear + mlp256 per the registered economy; train seeds {0,1,2}; estimand identical to S1 (per-prompt paired successes summed over train seeds, ONE prompt-cluster bootstrap CI95 over the 30 test prompts, n_boot 2000, seed 0). Critic recomputed BOTH grids' full decisions from the state files alone (margins, CIs, per-seed points, firing sets, R2, floors, OQ-6) — bit-exact on both, zero blockers — before either verdict landed. - finding: TELEMETRY EARNS EXISTENCE AT S3 AND THE PINNED REPLICATION CONFIRMS IT — the provisional qualifier lifts. This is the PURE ADDRESS-STREAM source the original hardware premise named (expert ids only — the story F-15 explicitly could not speak to). Primary grid: 7/12 offset->=2 (space, offset, capacity) triples fired all three registered conditions (CI95 lower > 0, all three train seeds positive, point >= +0.005), all 12 CI-positive: L1_olmoe_k16/off2 linear +0.0147 [+0.0113, +0.0182] and mlp256 +0.0200 [+0.0167, +0.0232]; L2_olmoe_k512/off2 +0.0206 [+0.0171, +0.0244] and off3 +0.0051 [+0.0036, +0.0066]; L3_olmoe_groups_v1/off2 +0.0721 [+0.0652, +0.0792], off3 +0.0419 [+0.0338, +0.0502], off4 +0.0207 [+0.0145, +0.0269]. Replication on fresh seeds: the PINNED gate L1_olmoe_k16/off2/linear RE-FIRED — +0.0173 [+0.0133, +0.0221], per-seed +0.0180/+0.0175/+0.0163 — and the ENTIRE FIRING SET reproduced cell-for-cell: the same 7 of 12 triples fired and the same 5 did not (verified by independent recomputation), margins agreeing across runs within 0.006 (median ~0.002; headline L3/off2 +0.0721 -> +0.0730 [+0.0646, +0.0813]; L2/off2 +0.0206 -> +0.0207; widest gap L3/off3 +0.0419 -> +0.0364). STRUCTURE, reproduced on both datasets and NOT S1's shape: margins concentrate at offset 2 and GROW with label coarse-graining (L3 > L2 > L1 at off2 on both runs); L1's margin is DEAD by offset 3 (points +0.0020/+0.0022, below the materiality floor — where S1's L1 off3 fired), so the demonstrated horizon is offset 2 in L1, offset 3 in L2 (point clears the floor on both runs, CI-lower below it on both — edge, disclosed), offset 4 in L3. FREE-INFORMATION COLLAPSE IS IMMEDIATE at S3: the free baseline (a) sits essentially ON the glue-majority floor already at offset 2 (L1 acc(a) 0.5811-0.5819 vs floor 0.5803 primary; 0.5869-0.5876 vs 0.5865 replication) — router telemetry is nearly the only thing lifting the probe off the floor (acc(c) 0.5954-0.5969 primary, 0.6032-0.6056 replication; +1.5-1.9 points). NET: the MoE router address stream — expert IDS alone, the cheapest hardware-observable signal in the taxonomy — carries short-horizon future-concept information that the decoder's free stream does not, at +0.006..+0.073 top-1 over floors of 0.42-0.59. K-2's registered existence question is answered YES for S3. Existence, not headroom. Per-source scorecard: S1 YES (value-aware compute tier, F-15/F-16), S3 YES (pure address stream, this finding), S2 (KV pages) unrun.
- caveats: EXISTENCE != ACTUATOR — K-3 (conversion to acceptance) is untouched and remains THE load-bearing open risk. CONFIRMATORY WEIGHT: the multiplicity exposure was owned at ~26% family-wise (12 uncorrected triples) pre-replication and the insurance bought was for the PINNED CELL ONLY — the 7/7 firing-set match is descriptive breadth, not seven independent confirmations. L2/off3 RIDES THE MATERIALITY EDGE ON BOTH RUNS (point +0.005145 / +0.006169 vs floor 0.005; CI-lower +0.003633 / +0.004396 below it; the registered rule gates condition (iii) on the point margin): both verdicts are robust to dropping the triple outright, and disclosure-only boot-seed scans 1..10 keep every fired CI-lower positive on both runs (replication gate range [+0.0132, +0.0135], edge cell [+0.0042, +0.0044]). NON-FIRING PROFILE NOT IDENTICAL: L1/off3/mlp256 was CI-positive-immaterial on primary (+0.0020 [+0.0005, +0.0035]) but fails all three conditions on replication (+0.0007 [-0.0011, +0.0022], one seed negative) — same firing set, weaker non-firing tail. GLUE-FLOOR DOMINANCE: the majority class IS the glue bucket in all three spaces on both harvests (L1 0.5803/0.5865, L2 0.4214/0.4276, L3 = L2 by construction) — achievable margins are compressed by a dominant none-of-the-concepts class; OQ-13's floor-reduction menu (glue-conditioned readouts, glue-split label spaces, content-dense prompts) is the registered-secondary path and is UNRUN. L3's +0.0721/+0.0730 reads through its recorded lopsided lens (two mega-groups covering ~461/510 concepts incl. all 20 zero-degree buckets; leading sizes 193 and 268). OQ-6 h-a is CI-crossing at off2 and CI-NEGATIVE at off3/off4 on BOTH runs (primary -0.0026 [-0.0071, +0.0021] / -0.0111 / -0.0134; replication -0.0042 [-0.0094, +0.0015] / -0.0173 / -0.0122; h sits BELOW (a) at off3/4) — registered wording guards hold: a statement about the 2048-dim linear budget, never an information bound on the tap. MECHANISM WITHHELD BY REGISTRATION: the S3 P1-analog (prefix-topic control) is pinned CONDITIONAL on this confirmation and is now OWED — no anticipatory-vs-topic-state language attaches to S3 until it runs (F-16's topic-state rejection is S1-scoped). Population: the 30 test prompt clusters are the registered population — the replication resamples generations and probe training, NOT prompts; regime is short factual prompts, one model, one tap design, the NF4-with-bf16-routers load regime only. Cosmetic payload strings disclosed: decision.rule carries the runner's hardwired S1 registration date (2026-08-30) while the S3 registration is 2026-08-31 (the payloads' top-level registered key cites it correctly), and both payloads print the runner's generic "EXISTENCE (provisional: replication ... required)" verdict string mechanically — S1-replication precedent; the registration's pinned branch supersedes it on the replication run. Payload provenances -dirty with untracked-only dirt, operator-attested (audit provenance d8cb7a0-dirty: it ran before the commit that landed it). No wall-clock claims; no acceptance claims; no F-1-style conversion implied.

## F-18 — The S3 address-stream margin is not prefix-topic state at the histogram level: P1-SURVIVES on both harvests — topic features close ~none of the free gap at token/concept level, the router margin passes the equal-dims control intact, and the one real topic component (L3 groups) is ~1/10 the tap's margin there (post-hoc P1-analog at S3)
- date: 2026-09-01
- regime: REAL MODELS (post-hoc diagnostic P1-analog at S3; NON-CRITERION by registration — cannot amend F-17's verdict) — the conditional pin in the S3 registration (2026-08-31) fired at F-17; instantiation registered 2026-09-01 in the BLUEPRINT "S3 P1-ANALOG INSTANTIATION" block AFTER F-17 landed and BEFORE any registered S3 P1 cell ran, with a dated in-block disclosure of the guard-permitted smoke (12 L1/off2 arms to a scratch out path, numbers visible before the block committed; every decision element derives from the committed conditional pin plus the committed grid payloads' provenance, none from the smoke; the smoke was later verified bit-exact against the registered primary state — an accidental determinism check). Instrument: tools/run_posthoc_topic.py source-parametrized (every new argument defaults to the committed S1 instrument — defaults asserted by tests, full-path neutrality critic-verified at the pre-run gate; a default rerun reproduces the committed S1 NUMBERS but not bytes, provenance gains feature_dims_recorded + args keys; bind_spaces_to_harvest + --n-slots binding on the load path; a cross-source primary lookup returns INCOMPLETE, never a silent verdict). Feature set (t) = 560 decoder-free dims from tokens[row, :anchor] ONLY, over the OLMoE alphabets (dims recorded in payload feature_dims_recorded, none asserted: the OLMoE alphabets merge to the same 511 k512-concepts-incl.-glue / 16 as S1, so t = 560 again; only the slots block differs — 1024 = 16 gates x 64 experts). Grid: {L1,L2,L3}_olmoe x offsets {2,3} x {t, at, abt, atn} x LINEAR x train seeds {0,1,2} = 72 cells per harvest, BOTH committed S3 harvests (primary seeds 0..7, runtime 14,265.9 s; replication seeds 8..11, 7,202.9 s; payload runtime >= sum of cell runtimes on both — fresh single passes, no resumed cells); (atn) = (a)+(t)+alignment-destroyed expert vectors (anchor (a+17) mod row-length), dims equal to (abt) by construction. Same estimand/bootstrap as Phase 3 (seed-summed prompt-cluster bootstrap, n_boot 2000, seed 0, 30 test clusters); M3/M4/(t)-(b) pair against the COMMITTED S3 grid state files via their sha headers + the 1e-12 payload-reproduction gate before anything trains. Runner commit f3bb32e-dirty on both payloads, where the dirt IS the instrument + registration that ran (reviewed at the pre-run critic gate, committed together with this finding; the smoke bit-exactness and the recompute make silent drift immaterial to the numbers). Critic recomputed ALL SIX readout families and BOTH conclusion labels from the state files alone (fresh implementation, zero imports from the repo): max abs deviation 0.000e+00 across every margin, CI bound, and per-seed point; grid identity exact (1 sha header + 72 unique cells each, 30 clusters per cell, state order == grid order); sha chain closed (posthoc state headers == committed grid headers == harvest metas; phase3 and label-space shas match disk and the grid payloads); ZERO BLOCKERS.

- finding: MECHANICAL LABEL: P1-SURVIVES on the primary harvest by the pinned rule; the replication harvest's identical mechanical label is confirmatory color only (the pinned rule gates on the primary harvest alone). The topic-state explanation of F-17's margin is REJECTED at the histogram family level, with one honest qualifier at L3. (i) THE PRIMARY CELL FIRES: M2 = (abt)-(at) at L1_olmoe_k16/off2/linear = +0.012958 [+0.008783, +0.016809], per-seed +0.012667/+0.013356/+0.012852 — CI-lower > 0, all three seeds positive, point >= +0.005. Replication: +0.017465 [+0.013190, +0.022209]; row-wise |delta| vs primary <= 0.0045 (M2) / <= 0.0051 (M2n). (ii) (t) ADDS ~NOTHING AT TOKEN/CONCEPT LEVEL: all four L1/L2 M3 = (at)-(a) rows are CI-crossing with |point| <= 0.00094 on the primary harvest — the prefix's concept histograms close essentially none of the free gap in the spaces where F-17's gate lives. (iii) THE QUALIFIER — L3 CARRIES A SMALL REAL TOPIC COMPONENT: (t) lifts off the floor at the group lens (t-cell gaps +0.0073..+0.0096 primary, +0.0038..+0.0057 replication) and M3 at L3/off3 clears the materiality floor outright on primary: +0.006972 [+0.003302, +0.011127], all seeds positive (L3/off2 +0.004917 [+0.002698, +0.007673] CI-positive below floor; replication L3 rows CI-positive below floor). The tap's L3 margin is ~10x that component: M2 at L3/off2 +0.067859 [+0.061060, +0.074616], M2n +0.071488. Consistently, M4 = (c)-(at) equals F-17's (c)-(a) minus exactly M3 (identity critic-verified to 1e-6): within 9.4e-4 of F-17's margins at L1/L2, ~0.005-0.007 lower at L3. (iv) THE EQUAL-DIMS CONTROL CONFIRMS: M2n = (abt)-(atn) is CI-positive in all six primary rows (+0.002631..+0.071488), 5/6 material — same 5/6 pattern as M2, L1/off3 the immaterial cell in both families; the measured pure-dilution cost M2noise = (atn)-(at) spans -0.0108..+0.0015 across both harvests (a WIDER band than S1's -0.0045..+3.3e-5; L3/off3 replication CI-negative at -0.010841 [-0.016374, -0.005024]) — dilution again depresses M2 rather than inflating it, and M2n - M2 = -M2noise holds per cell. (v) Descriptive: telemetry-alone beats topic-alone in all 12 rows on both harvests ((t)-(b) primary -0.1131..-0.0028). NET: what the OLMoE router addresses carry about offsets 2-3 is NOT recoverable from the decoder's generated-token history at the histogram level — the mechanism language F-17 withheld is now permitted in this registered form: the S3 signal lives in the model's computation, not its emitted text, except for a small (~1/10 at the coarse group lens) genuinely topic-shaped share.
- caveats: NON-CRITERION: this prices F-17's signal; it cannot amend F-17's verdict. "M2/M2n fire 6/6" is true ONLY under a CI-positive reading — 5/6 rows clear the +0.005 floor in each family, and L1/off3 is CI-positive-immaterial in both (M2 +0.002069 [+0.000503, +0.003900]; M2n +0.002631 [+0.001169, +0.004487]) — F-17's L1/off3 profile again; the replication M2n at L1/off3 is the one non-reproducing row, CI-crossing with one negative seed (+0.000883 [-0.002524, +0.003312], per-seed +0.00201/-0.00030/+0.00094) — the weaker tail, disclosed as at F-17. DATED ERRATUM (2026-09-01) to the instantiation block's disclosure (3), which reads "Dims differ from S1's 560/3266": the recorded t dim is 560, IDENTICAL to S1 (the OLMoE alphabets merge to the same 511/16); only the slots block differs (1024 vs S1's 2048), and the "3266" half inherits F-16's own 3266->3265 erratum — the block text stands, corrected here per the erratum convention. The payloads' registered/conclusion.rule strings date the P1 template (2026-08-31), not this instantiation (2026-09-01) — the same cosmetic residue class F-17 disclosed for decision.rule. The (atn) reading carries VERBATIM from F-16: the shifted-slots arm could in principle carry aligned-equivalent signal, a channel whose bias could only make M2n an UNDERestimate — SURVIVES rides on M2 and is unaffected. PROMPT-TOKEN RESIDUAL as registered: (t) omits prompt tokens (the harvest stores generated tokens only); a failure would have deflated a fortiori, survival leaves the prompt-augmented prefix control as the formally open residual and the cheapest next experiment if mechanism language is ever contested. HISTOGRAM FAMILY ONLY: richer prefix representations (sequence models over the prefix, k-NN/embedding similarity) fall under the residual, not this rejection; any richer control is a new registration. Offsets {2,3} ran verbatim per the template although F-17 establishes L1 signal at off2 only — off3 rows reported, no cell swap, conclusion weight on the pinned primary cell. Linear probes only (registered economy); 30-cluster power; L3 mega-group lens (two groups carry ~461/510 of the concept mass — F-17 carry-forward); glue-majority floors compress all margins in every space (F-17 carry-forward). No acceptance or wall-clock claims; K-3 untouched — this says the S3 signal is real and not derivable from emitted tokens at this family level, not that it converts.
- DATED NOTE (2026-09-06; F-24): the shift-17 equal-dims twin used here as the noise control is NOT inert under a null direction — it reads a position whose forward pass has consumed the label token for about 94% of anchors and gains CI-clear over the base at the OQ-17 gate on the primary harvest (Mn +0.0030 [+0.0014, +0.0046]; replication +0.0012 [-0.0004, +0.0026], straddling); the "could only make M2n an UNDERestimate" caveat holds under SURVIVES only. The mechanism reading of this finding is moot for the L1 offset 2 margin at the registered reader (a margin below the materiality floor and not beyond the equal-dims twin has no mechanism to price); the histogram-level rejection of topic-state as an explanation of the PROXY margin is untouched. Not rewritten.

## F-19 — K-3 Attempt #1 NULL: adaptive block scheduling on frozen telemetry probes does not convert the S1 probe margins into tokens-per-target-call at matched drafting budget — every gate leg fails on the fresh verdict population (telemetry vs vanilla +0.007 against a +0.062 bar, CI straddling zero), the pairwise budget guard breached twice by MECHANISM, and the information-free gamma-6 control confirms the gaming arithmetic the guard existed to block
- date: 2026-09-03
- regime: REAL MODELS (Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct target + Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct draft, NF4, RTX 3080 Ti). The registered Phase-4 K-3 Attempt #1 arc executed end to end: arc registration + amendments #1/#2, D2/D3 diagnostics, stage-E clarifications E1-E12, tolerance amendment, threshold-search registration (+ dated erratum + robustness contingency), void execution, fold-back count amendment — every block dated before the result it governs. Arms: 1 vanilla gamma-4; 2 draft-confidence (q-based stop/extend, the incumbent-style comparator); 3 (a)-probe free-signal; 4 (c)-probe telemetry (the K-3 bet); 5 fixed gamma-6 descriptive control. Thresholds frozen on the 30 val prompts by the registered constrained-max frontier search (97 evals, all ratios critic-recomputed bit-exact from state files; selections arm 2 stop q0.25/extend q0.5, arm 3 q0.95/q0.05, arm 4 q0.4/q0.05, re-frozen q0.35/q0.05 under the void). Verdict population = data/prompts_k3_verdict.jsonl (210 fresh committed prompts, authored before any D2/D3 output was read). Estimand = pooled emitted tokens per verify call (E6); delta = stats.paired_ratio_delta_ci (joint cluster bootstrap, n_boot 2000, seed 0). WIN gate (all-hold): for EVERY comparator in {1,2,3}: pairwise delta CI95 lower > 0 AND point >= +0.062 AND arm-4 drafted-per-emitted <= comparator + 0.01.
- VERDICT (seed-1 void-rerun, the registered single rerun): NOT WIN. arm4-vs-arm1 delta +0.006860 CI95 [-0.015024, +0.025572]; arm4-vs-arm3 +0.006974 [-0.014909, +0.025690]; arm4-vs-arm2 +0.048863 [-0.007393, +0.104327] with the budget guard breached a SECOND time (arm 4 dpe 1.317033 vs allowed 1.285681; margin +0.0314, vs seed-0 +0.0289). Every leg fails BOTH statistical criteria (CI95 lower > 0; point >= +0.062); the arm-2 leg additionally breaches the guard, while the arm-1/arm-3 guards are satisfied (margins -0.0173/-0.0174) — the null is not a guard technicality. Seed-1 pooled tokens/call: vanilla 3.020427 (dpe 1.324316), draft-conf 2.978424 (1.275681), (a)-probe 3.020313 (1.324403), telemetry 3.027287 (1.317033), gamma-6 3.428206 (1.750186).
- SHRINKAGE, measured: the telemetry arm's val-tuned advantage (+0.060 over vanilla on the 30 tuning prompts, itself a max over 32 search evals) shrank to +0.007 on fresh prompts — the expected direction for a maximum taken over 32 tuning evals on 30 prompts. The voided seed-0 run shows the same null independently: arm 4 t/c 3.042553 vs vanilla 3.032023 (+0.0105). Two seeds, one story.
- VOID STORY (disclosed per registration): the seed-0 verdict runs breached the arm-2 leg (arm 4 dpe 1.313115 vs allowed 1.284202); gate metrics were quarantined unread (budget columns only consulted; run stdout never opened); the registered re-freeze could shave only 0.000556 of val budget (the 32 committed arm-4 evals span dpe 1.2485-1.2912); the pinned honest prediction — likely re-breach — CONFIRMED at seed 1. Mechanism (both seeds): val->verdict budget inflation vanilla +0.042/+0.047, arm 3 +0.048/+0.053, arm 4 +0.064/+0.068, arm 2 -0.0005/+0.001 — q-based stopping self-regulates under population difficulty while the probe arm's extend-open channel (extend threshold at the q0.05 grid edge, selected by the val frontier because extends were budget-cheap AND t/c-positive on val) inflates. DESIGN LESSON, carried: matched val budget caps do NOT guarantee matched verdict budgets across mechanistically different stopping policies; a pairwise guard priced for noise (epsilon 0.01 vs measured breach ~0.03) becomes the binding constraint when comparator budgets self-regulate. Voided seed-0 arm 5: t/c 3.443888 (dpe 1.742217).
- CONTROL ARITHMETIC: the committed gamma-4 accepted-length distribution P(k=0..4) = 0.2509/0.1751/0.1341/0.0953/0.3445 gives E[emitted/call] 3.1071 (registered: reproduces the measured ~3.11); its geometric-tail extension (a = 0.3445^(1/4) = 0.7661) predicts always-extend gamma-6 t/c 3.6134 vs measured 3.4282 (+0.185 over-prediction — geometric-tail optimism, disclosed) — direction confirmed: raw tokens/call is buyable information-free at +0.43 dpe, which is why the matched-budget frame and pairwise guard were load-bearing, and why arm 5 is descriptive and never in the gate minimum.
- ANALYTIC ESTIMAND (Amendment #1's dated demotion: REPORTED, not gating — the registered promise closed here): seed-1 pooled analytic drafted acceptance — vanilla 0.734390, draft-conf 0.745370, (a)-probe 0.734337, telemetry 0.734971, gamma-6 0.731349. Concordant with the null (telemetry minus vanilla ~ +0.0006) and displaying exactly the composition confound the demotion named: arm 2's higher value reflects its drafted-position mix under aggressive stopping, not superior drafting.
- CONTINGENCY: the arm-2 robustness contingency (erratum block) did NOT fire — it triggers only on a PASSING arm4-vs-arm2 leg. The arm-2 under-tuning residual (~0.027 val t/c, anti-conservative) is therefore moot for this verdict: the leg failed anyway.
- P-STAGE RECORD (seed 0, standing): fold-back precondition evaluated before the void — arm3-vs-arm1 +0.000451 CI95 [-0.001952, +0.003220], NO FOLD-BACK; arm 3 is a near-exact vanilla null (its q0.95 stop rarely fires — the degenerate-tuning disclosure). Timing disclosure (critic): the seed-0 arm-4 run launched 25 s before the NO-FOLD-BACK commit landed — the fold-back eval preceded the launch in-session, but committed evidence alone cannot prove that ordering; moot for validity (the precondition passed; the counterfactual is a cost deviation only). Meta note: seed-1 arm-2 binds commit 48814ac-dirty, arms 1/3/4/5 bind 92fa9cb-dirty — a docs-only delta (identical code; probe-weight and prompt-encoding shas identical across all five arms).
- CARRIES/DISCLOSURES (accumulated, registered at their dates): E2-vs-D-7 glue-undefined agreement semantics tension; depressed realized trigger rates (quantiles frozen on harvest-time signals shift live); arm-3 tuning degenerate (all-e0 frontier, grid-edge winner, span inside single-eval noise); the arm-2 selection erratum (frontier argument inverted in realization; selection stood, in-noise); arm-4 s2 exclusion margins +1.083e-4..+2.87e-3 (second realized inversion, outcome-irrelevant); containment evidence scope (one GPU/kernel stack, vanilla arm); arm-2 search evals ran reader-free; two of 16 tap-rider anchors shared with the calibration sample; mean-of-prompts vs pooled acceptance labeling; the seed-0 arm-1-3 records are the P-stage record while the seed-1 records are the gate comparators (P-stage "no rerun" sentence superseded under void, dated).
- KILL TEXT: this is K-3 firing exactly as registered — probe margins (F-15/F-17 existence, both real) did not convert to acceptance-side throughput through this actuator at this pair/scale. Per the FOLD-BACK COUNT AMENDMENT (2026-09-02, critic-gated; it WIDENED the kill text's original single fold-back to the structured two-lane sequence, a dated pre-verdict-read change disclosed in its own text): fold-back #1 = OQ-15 (outcome-supervised acceptance head; its arc registration must pin the exhaustive terminal-outcome partition before running); an information-ceiling null there skips OQ-16 and stops the lane; a conversion-mode null hands off to OQ-16 (vector reconstruction -> SD^2 injection); hard stop after #2 or the skip. SALVAGE per the kill doc: the per-offset gating rules and staleness map stand; the byproduct logs (per-block telemetry + outcomes + anchor states + analytic acceptance, ALL arms, BOTH seeds, ~10 runs x 210 prompts) are the OQ-15/OQ-16 training data, collected for free by the registered amendment. No wall-clock claims (D-9); no acceptance-rate claims beyond the labeled tokens/call estimand.
