## F-20 — OQ-15 Stage I: the registered existence gate passes mechanically, but the registered stale-target control (five permutation seeds) leaves the margin indistinguishable at this power from zero-information target draws (pinned label ARTIFACT-DOMINANT) — block-aligned acceptance-relevant telemetry information above free signals is NOT demonstrated, and the telemetry head loses outright to the free-features head despite its +2048 extra dims
- date: 2026-09-03
- regime: REAL MODELS (Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct target + Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct draft, NF4, RTX 3080 Ti; S1-style value-tier slot telemetry, 4 layers x top-32). CPU-only analysis of the K-3 arm-1 (vanilla gamma-4) byproduct logs, both verdict seeds (17,673 + 17,581 blocks x 4 drafted offsets) + val/tapcal runs as TRAIN-only rows. Registered pre-run (OQ-15 FOLD-BACK #1 ARC REGISTRATION, commit 89b38ef): prompt-level split default_rng(15) -> 126/42/42, target = per-position ANALYTIC acceptance 1 - TV(p_j, q_j) (D-9 headline form, not survival-conditioned), features via the committed scheduler.probe_input (FREE 657 dims + q_sig availability rule; TEL +2048 slot multi-hot; NOISE = F-16's alignment-destroying shift), closed-form ridge with lambda-on-VAL the only DOF, gate = delta_noise = pooled TEST MSE(NOISE) - MSE(TEL) via stats.paired_cluster_delta_ci (42 prompt clusters pooling both seed-runs, 28,616 positions, n_boot 2000, seed 0). The DI-1 stale-target control was critic-proposed at the recompute round and REGISTERED with pinned conclusion labels BEFORE it ran (the P1 post-hoc pattern).
- STAGE-I GATE (mechanical): PASS. delta_noise = +0.000763 CI95 [+0.000308, +0.001215]. Critic recompute from the state rows: bit-exact by two independent bootstrap implementations, and a full deterministic rerun of the tool from the sha-verified raws reproduced summary and state BYTE-IDENTICAL. Timeline clean (registration commit 02:50, run 03:35).
- DI-1 STALE-TARGET CONTROL (the meaning check): with every (run, prompt)'s targets replaced by a seeded random permutation of its own block rows — no block-aligned information available to ANY arm, prompt-level statistics preserved, lambda re-selected inside the control — the TEL-vs-NOISE margin persists at +0.000643 CI95 [-0.000076, +0.001357] (primary, ~84% of the live point) and +0.000276 [-0.000424, +0.000943] (replicate). Pinned labels: primary point >= 0.5*d_live -> ARTIFACT-DOMINANT; replicate -> ARTIFACT-NOT-EXCLUDED; the pinned disagreement rule carries ARTIFACT-DOMINANT. SEED-SPREAD EXTENSION (registered before running, reported-only, label unchanged by its own pin): perm seeds 2/3/4 land at +0.000164 [-0.000469, +0.000810], -0.000713 [-0.001236, -0.000200] (CI-NEGATIVE), and -0.000105 [-0.000733, +0.000557] — all three below half, so the extension's registered honest-weight sentence fires. REFINED READING the five seeds force: the redundancy channel shows no evidence of systematic bias at n=5 (stale mean +0.000053, SE ~0.00023 — a systematic component up to roughly half the live margin remains consistent with these points) and manifests as DRAW VARIANCE of the live margin's own magnitude (sd ~ 0.0005); no stale cell reproduces a CI-positive pooled delta, and the live point exceeds all five stale points (rank 1 of 6, one-sided rank p ~ 0.17) while sitting ~1.4 sd above the stale mean. The control therefore leaves the existence claim UNSUPPORTED-BUT-NOT-REFUTED: indistinguishable, at this power, from the zero-information draw distribution. The primary control also produced a CI-positive per-offset cell on the random target (off3 +0.00172 [+0.00022, +0.00322]) — consistent with the same-anchor-redundancy channel alone generating "significant" deltas of the gate's magnitude, though the cell does not replicate (replicate off3 -0.00033 [-0.00164, +0.00104]; one of 8 subgroup CIs — the pooled primary point is the demonstration, the subgroup cell is color) — and every stale head lands worse than the trivial train-mean baseline, exactly as an unpredictable target should produce. MECHANISM (the registered CANDIDATE, demoted by the spread): TEL's slot dims are same-anchor telemetry, partially linearly redundant with the anchor's own 657 free dims, while NOISE's donor-block slots are only prompt-level redundant — the registration named this as a systematic variance-cost asymmetry, but the seed spread did not confirm the systematic-mean form; what survives measured is the draw variance, which the redundancy asymmetry may still shape.
- MANDATED SIGN-DISAGREEMENT DISCUSSION (the registration's own clause fires): delta_free = MSE(FREE) - MSE(TEL) = -0.00286 CI95 [-0.00322, -0.00252] — decisively negative at every offset (-0.0019..-0.0039). The telemetry head does not survive its own +2048-dim dilution: the free-features head is the better deployable predictor outright. Test MSEs 0.0423 (FREE) / 0.0451 (TEL) / 0.0459 (NOISE) against trivial baseline ~0.0538 pooled: the free features explain ~+0.0115; the disputed above-noise-twin increment is ~7% of that.
- PER-OFFSET + SIDEBAR STRUCTURE: live delta_noise is carried by off1 (+0.00087 [+0.00023, +0.00150]) and off3 (+0.00118 [+0.00014, +0.00224]); off2 is a point-zero straddle (-0.00005) and off4 straddles (+0.00105 [-0.00011, +0.00216]). The survival-conditioned binary sidebar (labeled per D-9) does not corroborate: FREE beats TEL on AUC at all four offsets (0.772/0.771/0.555/0.541 vs 0.738/0.723/0.516/0.519) and NOISE beats TEL at off2/off3; free-features selective prediction is real at early offsets (off1 accept-tail precision 0.967 at coverage 0.20; reject-tail 0.552 vs ~0.27 base) and near-trivial at off3/off4 — the SpecDec++-class free signal is the strong early-position predictor here, and q_sig sits INSIDE the free set.
- DISCLOSURES: all 24 chosen lambdas sat at the grid edge (10.0; the F-19 grid-edge precedent — selection saturated, heads likely under-regularized, symmetric across arms so the pairing survives). DI-1 scale caveat: the stale target's predictable variance is lower, so the control bounds the artifact channel's presence, not its exact live share. SCHEMA RESIDUE (critic blocker): the DI-1 diagnostic cells print the gate schema's stage1_pass/branch fields mechanically — the "INFORMATION-CEILING NULL" strings inside the rotcheck summaries name a partition branch DI-1 is forbidden to declare and carry NO lane-state meaning; only the registered primary out (oq15_stage1.json) carries the partition. PROVENANCE: the DI-1 registration, its instrument change, and this finding land in the same commit — the rotcheck metas bind 89b38ef-dirty, the dirt IS that in-flight material (the F-17/F-18 attestation pattern); the in-session ordering (registration text before control run) is operator-attested, and content-verified in that every pinned threshold derives from d_live alone, which was known before any control ran. Noise self-map sliver: 0 prompt-runs. Pre-registration structural data contact disclosed in the registration block.
- FORMAL STATE + WHAT THIS MEANS: per the DI-1 registration the mechanical PASS stands (non-criterion; no registered text lets the control reclassify the partition branch, and post-readout amendments to gating pins are prohibited by the registration itself). Stage C therefore remains AUTHORIZED and USER-GATED. What the control removes is the pass's registered meaning: the claim "the stream carries block-aligned acceptance-relevant information above free + prompt-level statistics" is NOT SUPPORTED — the margin is indistinguishable at this power from the zero-information draw distribution (whose spread the primary cell happened to sample from the top of). The honest evidence summary for the Stage-C cost decision: the TEL head loses to the FREE head on held-out prediction everywhere, and a Stage-C WIN requires the TEL-head arm to beat the FREE-head arm by +0.062 tokens/call at matched budget. METHOD LESSON, carried (extends F-16 practice): an equal-dims noise anchor prices dilution but NOT the zero-information draw distribution of small margins under same-anchor redundancy — and a SINGLE stale draw misleads in either direction (the primary seed alone read as a systematic artifact; seed 3 alone would have read as clean). When the feature block under test is redundant with the base features it accompanies, pair the anchor with a MULTI-SEED stale-target (permuted-outcome) control before reading a small CI-positive margin as information. F-15/F-17 are not obviously threatened: different readout (classification accuracy vs ridge MSE), margins ~20x this one, and — the load-bearing argument — the variance-cost channel shrinks roughly as dims/n and the Phase-3 probes trained on ~30-70x more anchors than these heads' ~25k rows; a stale-label analog there is the cheap check if this is ever contested. F-19's null stands.

## F-21 — OQ-15 Stage C (fold-back #1 terminal): CONVERSION-MODE NULL — the outcome-supervised telemetry head does not convert to tokens-per-call at matched budget on the fresh verdict population: it ties vanilla (+0.003, CI straddling zero) and loses CI-clear to the free-features head arm (-0.082), the live-conversion mirror of F-20's delta_free; the seed-2 guard breach was a selection-time budget gap (not inflation), resolved by the registered void semantics with the guard clearing every leg at seed 3
- date: 2026-09-04
- regime: REAL MODELS (Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct target + Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct draft, NF4, RTX 3080 Ti). The registered OQ-15 Stage-C conversion stage executed end to end under the arc registration (commit 89b38ef) + STAGE-C FREEZE amendment (9e8fa4a) + STAGE-C VOID EXECUTION (46ab439) — every block dated before the result it governs. Arms: C1 vanilla gamma-4; C2 draft-confidence (the incumbent-style comparator, K-3 arm-2 policy retuned on fresh val); C3 = head_free (outcome-head policy on FREE features, 657 dims — the matched-actuator free baseline); C4 = head_tel (the OQ-15 bet: same policy on FREE+TEL, 2705 dims). Head policy: STOP at consult j in {1,2} iff S_j = prod a_hat_1..j < tau_stop[j] (strict); EXTEND to gamma 6 iff S_4*a_hat_5 >= tau_ext; a_hat from the frozen ridge heads — offsets 1-4 Stage-I, offset 5 trained at freeze time on the arm-5 byproducts (oq15_head_weights.npz, sha 35754b8a2b05.., binding-gated export bit-exact vs the committed Stage-I state). Estimand = pooled emitted tokens per verify call (E6); delta = stats.paired_ratio_delta_ci (joint cluster bootstrap, n_boot 2000, seed 0). WIN gate (all-hold): for EVERY comparator in {C1, C2, C3}: pairwise delta CI95 lower > 0 AND point >= +0.062 AND C4 drafted-per-emitted <= comparator's + 0.01. Containment re-validated pre-verdict (oq15c_containment_rider.json, PASS on the strictest bit-identity branch).
- VERDICT (seed-3 void-rerun, the registered single rerun): NOT WIN — every leg fails BOTH statistical criteria, and NO leg fails on the guard (arm 7's dpe 1.2742347176420175 sits below all three comparators outright: 1.3296655675930287 / 1.3170482039828773 / 1.2999498131935538) — the null is not a guard technicality. C4-vs-C1 +0.002664 CI95 [-0.044719, +0.046727]; C4-vs-C2 -0.050425 [-0.109388, +0.005772]; C4-vs-C3 -0.081846 [-0.132484, -0.035567] — CI-CLEAR NEGATIVE. Seed-3 pooled tokens/call: vanilla 3.00827523663776 (dpe 1.3296655675930287), draft-conf 3.061364024841889 (1.3170482039828773), head_free 3.0927852831273355 (1.2999498131935538), head_tel 3.010939077751599 (1.2742347176420175).
- VOID STORY (disclosed per registration): the seed-2 verdicts breached the arm-6 (C3) budget leg — arm 7 dpe 1.312796030373656 vs allowance 1.2973549568599316 + 0.01, breach 0.00544107351372447 (~0.54x epsilon); arm-1/arm-2 legs complied. Gate metrics were quarantined unread (budget columns only; arms 1/2 seed-2 tokens/call held unread too, beyond the letter; one disclosed comparator exposure — arm 6's pooled summary in the chain's stdout tail, seen pre-guard-evaluation, gate-inert under supersession). MECHANISM, and it differs from F-19's: not val->verdict inflation (arm 7 held budget +0.0008) but a SELECTION-TIME pairwise gap — constrained maximization under the common B_ref cap left arm 7 selecting 0.0136 below the cap and arm 6 selecting 0.0398 below it (val budgets 1.3119926682377585 vs 1.2858072916666667, gap 2.6x epsilon, frontier-grid discreteness), knowable at freeze time from the committed selection budgets and not noticed. RE-FREEZE (val evidence only; K-3's min-budget cap rule deliberately NOT transposed — it would have forced a degenerate floor cell, val t/c 1.830078125, on a search that walked the stop ladder to budget ~0.632): new cap = arm 6's own SELECTED val budget 1.2858072916666667, removing the val-time gap by construction; the registered selection key over the 18 newly-feasible committed evals forces a UNIQUE cell (stop q0.15, extend q0.75), stop_values [0.5404841213563928, 0.3435865072842257], extend_value 0.301154537587004, val t/c 2.96675791943684, val budget 1.266543633008173 -> oq15c_thresholds_arm7_v2.json (VOID_REFREEZE). PINNED HONEST PREDICTION, both halves CONFIRMED: guard clears (re-breach needed differential drift > +0.0293; realized -0.0065) and the conversion-null expectation strengthens (the re-freeze cut arm 7's val t/c by 0.093 against a +0.062 bar).

- ANALYTIC ESTIMAND (REPORTED, not gating — the owned deviation carried from F-19): seed-3 pooled analytic drafted acceptance — vanilla 0.7333714157215158, draft-conf 0.7383296680323516, head_free 0.739880945182453, head_tel 0.7416298732067561. Head_tel's top rank carries the SAME composition confound F-19 named: it drafts the fewest tokens (68,392 vs 69,936-70,765) under the most aggressive stopping (4,138 stop / 4,142 extend blocks of 17,826; head_free 2,824/3,966; draft-conf 2,916/4,160), so higher per-drafted-position acceptance does not convert to tokens/call — the conversion failure in one line.
- FINDING DUTIES (pinned at the freeze round, discharged): (i) EXTEND-SIGNAL BREAKDOWN: the offset-5 heads that gate every EXTEND land WORSE than the trivial train-mean baseline on TEST (free 0.05469985821808974 / tel 0.05974761296262184 vs trivial 0.052217), and the committed Stage-I per-head MSEs show tel_off3/off4 also worse than their per-offset baselines — the entire extend product beyond S_2 rides on factors with no demonstrated held-out advantage over constants (quantile threshold materialization cancels constants exactly), while only the stop consults rest on clearly-better-than-trivial heads. The registered actuator was therefore mostly steering on noise wherever it extended. (ii) GRID-EDGE: all ten exported heads (2 families x offsets 1-5) chose lambda 10.0, the grid edge (F-19/F-20 precedent; symmetric across arms). (iii) RNG-CHILDREN: spawn(2, 30) val-eval streams share SeedSequence children with the first 30 seed-2 verdict streams — different prompt files diverge immediately, no practical coupling; the seed-3 gate runs use spawn(3, .), fully independent. Plus: the v2 re-freeze moved arm 7 extend-OPEN (tau_ext 0.4036281867600829 -> 0.301154537587004; seed-2 v1 usage was near-inert at 1,341 stop / 1,745 extend blocks) — the operating point the gate measured is the more-active one, and it still lost; and every meta binds git_commit "-dirty" mechanically (untracked results accrue mid-chain; porcelain counts them — instrument artifact, code identical to the named commits).
- CONSISTENCY WITH F-20 + DESCRIPTIVE OBSERVATION: the C4-vs-C3 CI-negative leg is the conversion-level mirror of F-20's CI-negative delta_free (the TEL head loses to the FREE head on held-out prediction at every offset) — prediction-level and conversion-level readouts now agree with CIs clear of zero on both. Descriptive, NOT a registered leg, no delta CI claimed: head_free posted the highest pooled t/c on both seeds (3.0928 seed-3 / 3.0831 seed-2) at a budget BELOW vanilla's — a head_free-vs-vanilla claim would need its own registered readout (the K-3 arm-3 analog was a near-exact vanilla null, so this would be new territory, flagged only).
- GATE-TIME BINDING ASSERTS (pinned at the freeze + void rounds): PASS — arm-7 seed-3 meta binds thresholds_sha256 4197e064.. (the v2 file, selection_label VOID_REFREEZE), arms 2/6 bind their unchanged SELECTED v1 shas (b8fcaa16.. / cd030384..), all adaptive thresholds carry provenance.val_run == oq15c_val_arm1 (the old-K-3-thresholds foot-gun checked), both head arms bind head_weights_sha256 35754b8a.., all four runs at seed 3 on prompts_sha 71fc9995...
- TERMINAL CLASSIFICATION (by the pinned partition, mechanical): Stage I PASSED + Stage C NOT-WIN = CONVERSION-MODE NULL. Per the FOLD-BACK COUNT AMENDMENT this hands off to OQ-16 (fold-back #2: vector reconstruction -> SD^2 injection); the lane hard-stops after #2. OQ-16 is a NEW arc — its own registration and its own user GPU authorization; nothing runs from this classification. Honest weight for that decision, carried from F-20 and now conversion-confirmed: the telemetry stream's block-aligned information margin is indistinguishable from zero-information draws at Stage-I power, the TEL head loses to FREE at both prediction and conversion, and both OQ-15 nulls landed exactly where the registered honest expectations said they would. What survives for OQ-16's design: the actuator itself converts free signals respectably through this machinery (head_free descriptive observation above), so a genuine telemetry margin — if OQ-16's reconstruction target carries one — has a demonstrated-live actuator path; what does not survive is any expectation that S1-tier value-slot telemetry carries that margin.
- caveats: single model pair, single GPU/kernel stack, NF4 quantization; 210-prompt verdict population, 30-prompt val (frontier selections are maxima over ~41-44 evals on 30 prompts — the F-19 shrinkage mechanism applies to all tuned arms symmetrically); no wall-clock claims (D-9); analytic acceptance REPORTED only; the seed-2 exposure disclosure and beyond-the-letter quarantine practice are recorded in the void block; head policies never consult at block 1 (structural); stop thresholds STRICT inequality. The extend-signal breakdown means the measured null prices this actuator WITH its noise-gated extend channel; a stop-only actuator variant was never registered and is not claimed either way.

The audit's read-only replay of the logged decisions from the gitignored per-prompt byproduct arrays (not reproducible from the committed tree) matches every logged stop_pos / extend decision of the seed-3 verdict arms: 17,185 of 17,185 consulted blocks on arm 6 (C3, free features) and 17,616 of 17,616 on arm 7 (C4, telemetry). Standardized inputs on the consulted decision path reach |z| 17,531 (arm 6) and 122,318 (arm 7); 111 / 120 consulted blocks exceed |z| 100 at offset 1. The honest exposure measure is the counterfactual TRAIN-support clip (the DR-1 form): it changes 41 decisions on arm 6 (0.24% of consulted blocks) and 41 on arm 7 (0.23%), in both directions; the voided seed-2 arms read 41 / 30 of 16,970 / 17,257, and the OQ-20 seed-6 arms 42 / 47 of 17,157 / 17,602. A first-order estimate of the direct tokens/call effect is at most 0.0119 / 0.0116 (41 decisions times at most five drafted tokens, a stop at position 1 against an extension to six, over the consulted calls; the rest of a changed prompt's trajectory is not priced; probable, not rerun), far inside the C4-vs-C3 interval -0.081846 [-0.132484, -0.035567] and the vs-vanilla straddle, so CONVERSION-MODE NULL stands as read. Rule recorded in docs/LESSONS.md: a scorer that runs live carries its TRAIN support clip, pinned at the freeze, not only in diagnostics.

## F-22 — OQ-16 Stage R: STAGE-R PASS — the S1 telemetry stream linearly reconstructs the anchor hidden state h_a above both the alignment-destroyed twin (+0.176, ~85 stale-sd clear) and the free-features baseline (+0.205), the registered gate's first surprising branch and the refutation of its own pinned expectation; but the registered magnitudes are inflated ~25% by a standardization pathology on two out-of-support test prompts, the honest deployable margin against the mean predictor is NOT CI-clear at the pooled estimand, and the post-gate diagnostics DR-1/DR-2 (registered before running) price what the pass is worth
- date: 2026-09-04
- regime: REAL MODELS (Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct target + Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct draft, NF4, RTX 3080 Ti; S1-style value-tier slot telemetry, 4 layers x top-32 hash-sketched addresses at the down_proj inputs of layers 7/15/23/31). CPU-only analysis of the FOUR vanilla gamma-4 verdict byproduct runs (K-3 seeds 0/1 on data/prompts_k3_verdict.jsonl; OQ-15C seeds 2/3 on data/prompts_oq15_verdict.jsonl; 70,529 blocks; runs [0] and [2] are voided attempts' vanilla arms — logging was never quarantined, disclosed at registration). Registered pre-run (OQ-16 FOLD-BACK #2 ARC REGISTRATION, commit bd71358): target = the byproduct-logged anchor hidden state h_a (model.norm output, 4096 dims, float16 on disk — the cast is part of the target), z-scored per coordinate on pooled TRAIN moments; features = anchor-time core only through the committed scheduler.probe_input (FREE 657 / TEL 2705 / NOISE = F-16 shift 17; NO q_sig — it does not exist at the anchor, critic-verified in k3_live/hf_specdec); prompt-level 126/42/42 splits per population (Stage-I recipe), cluster = (population, prompt), 84 TEST clusters; multi-output closed-form ridge, lambda the only DOF over the WIDENED grid 1e-2..1e5; estimand = pooled TEST standardized MSE, deltas by stats.paired_ratio_delta_ci (per-cluster SSE / position counts, n_boot 2000, seed 0); GATE = two-leg AND: leg 1 delta_noise CI-lower > 0 AND point > stale_mean + 3 sd AND point > stale max over a GATING five-seed stale-target control (DI-1 recipe, lambda re-selected inside every control); leg 2 delta_free CI-lower > 0. Three design decisions put to the user before the registration (all-four-vanilla-runs / two-leg gate / direction proxy reported-only). Timeline: registration commit bd71358 -> registered run (no overrides, meta bd71358-dirty from the untracked HANDOFF only) -> critic attack round -> DR-1/DR-2 registered -> DR runs -> this finding. The DR-registration-before-DR-run ordering (registration text ~3.5 min before the runs' started_unix, ~25 min before any DR result) is provable from the session transcript only; the committed record binds the DR metas in the same commit as the text — operator-attested, the -dirty pattern.
- GATE (mechanical): STAGE-R PASS. Leg 1: delta_noise = +0.176469 CI95 [+0.130414, +0.251518]; the five stale-seed points +0.000343 / +0.000849 / -0.000698 / -0.004252 / +0.000294 (mean -0.000693, sd 0.002067, 3-sd bar +0.005508, max +0.000849) — the live point clears the bar by ~32x (the "~85 sd" figure is bar clearance against five permutation draws, not effect precision; the bootstrap CI is the precision statement). Leg 2: delta_free = +0.204984 [+0.153698, +0.288162]. Per population: k3 delta_noise +0.1285 [+0.1214, +0.1357] / delta_free +0.1532 [+0.1434, +0.1635]; oq15 +0.2235 [+0.1350, +0.3724] / +0.2558 [+0.1581, +0.4185]. TEST standardized MSE free 1.0880 / tel 0.8831 / noise 1.0595 vs trivial (train-mean) 0.9901 -> R^2 -0.099 / +0.108 / -0.070; centered-cosine medians 0.239 / 0.496 / 0.287 (standardized space); per-coordinate R^2 quartiles tel [+0.065, +0.123, +0.164], free and noise negative throughout; TEL below both baselines in 84/84 clusters. The pinned HONEST EXPECTATION (a dilution null — leg 2 failing as the same stream's delta_free failed at F-20) is REFUTED, recorded against it.
- CRITIC ATTACK ROUND (rule 7: the surprising branch gets the hardest scrutiny; all before any finding text): leakage CLEAN — the feature path reads SUMMARY_KEYS + slots only, h is logged by the norm catch and slots by the shadow tap from the same verify forward at the same position, the noise shift touches slots only, the stale permutation touches targets only, TRAIN-only moments reproduced from the raws (max diff 0); deterministic rerun BYTE-IDENTICAL (summary 10,356 B, state 178,069 B); fresh-implementation bootstrap bit-exact on all twelve deltas/CIs, per-population values and the label; sha binds intact; no kill-criteria dodge (partition pinned pre-run with the opposite expectation; a PASS authorizes only a user-gated stage).
- WHAT DID NOT SURVIVE — THE MAGNITUDES (critic B2): Standardizer.from_moments floors only exactly-zero sd, and rare mass512 buckets carry TRAIN sd down to ~1e-7. Two oq15 TEST prompts (orig 140, 37) put mass into such buckets and produce standardized inputs of 26,662 and 14,082; ridge at 1e5 cannot guard an unbounded input, and EVERY trained arm lands 4-8x worse than the mean predictor on those clusters (per-cluster MSE triv/tel/free/noise 0.969 / 5.760 / 8.265 / 8.084 and 0.975 / 3.495 / 4.704 / 4.529; 9.4% + 5.4% of pooled TEL SSE). Consequences, critic-recomputed: (i) TEL-vs-TRIVIAL pooled +0.10701 [-0.06983, +0.22088] — the honest deployable margin against a perfectly-shrunk baseline is NOT CI-clear at the registered pooled estimand (k3 +0.2163 [+0.2037, +0.2287] clear; oq15 -0.0002 [-0.3557, +0.2238]); TEL beats trivial in 81/84 clusters with median per-cluster gain +0.2243 (k3) / +0.2251 (oq15), the three losers being exactly the pathology prompts (140, 37, 206). (ii) The registered deltas are pathology-INFLATED, not deflated: the critic's UNREGISTERED drop-two diagnostic (quoted only as such) gives delta_noise +0.1327 [+0.1278, +0.1373], delta_free +0.1569 [+0.1504, +0.1629], TEL-vs-TRIVIAL +0.2162 [+0.2050, +0.2263], label still PASS — label robust, magnitudes ~a quarter pathology. (iii) Stage I (F-20) shared the standardization and was shielded by predict_clipped's [0,1] clip; the unbounded target removed the shield. (dated note 2026-10-16: the live Stage-C heads of F-21 ran with no support clip either; F-21's dated note of the same date records the replayed exposure, 41 changed decisions per arm under a counterfactual TRAIN-support clip) 4,063 TEST cells exceed 20 sd over the 657 free-feature columns (6,212 over the full TEL design) — the pathology is widespread at lower magnitude. The DR-1 clip is an OUT-OF-SUPPORT guard, not an sd floor: only 425 of those 6,212 cells lie outside TRAIN support, the clip changes 446 TEST cells in total, and ~5,800 in-support >20-sd cells remain (post-clip max |z| 204.1, ~sqrt(n_train)).
- DECOMPOSITION OF LEG 2 (exact identity on points): delta_free = delta_noise + (MSE_FREE - MSE_NOISE) = 0.176469 + 0.028515. NOISE-vs-FREE +0.02852 [+0.02319, +0.03542] (k3 +0.0247, oq15 +0.0323), and all five stale delta_free draws are CI-clear at +0.0368..+0.0400 — the prompt-level slot-statistics component (~14% of the free margin): genuine telemetry content at deployment, NOT block-aligned information, and not what leg 1 measures.
- GRID-EDGE DUTY (fourth consecutive event): all 18 lambda cells (3 arms x 6 variants) at 1e5, the TOP of the widened grid, with every VAL curve still descending (free live 1.0290 -> 0.9535 from 1e4 to 1e5; tel 0.8290 -> 0.8036). Leg 2 therefore compared TEL against a free arm capped short of its lambda->inf optimum (the trivial predictor) — the free arm's true reconstruction signal is near zero, and the registered +0.205 overstates the deployable free margin by roughly the free arm's own under-regularization (~0.10). Any Stage-A freeze must extend the grid to include the trivial predictor in every arm's family.
- RECONCILIATION WITH F-20/F-21 (no shared bug needed): the slot channel is proximal to h_a (the layer-31 tap is one linear map + residual + norm away from the target — critic A6; a layers-{7,15,23}-only ablation would separate "telemetry" from "the last MLP's addresses", reported-level) but blind to the draft-target mismatch that acceptance depends on. Losing at acceptance prediction (F-20 delta_free CI-negative) and at conversion (F-21) while winning at state identity are different targets, exactly the slice the registration named as its reason to run.
- DR-1 / DR-2 POST-GATE DIAGNOSTICS (registered with pinned labels and honest predictions AFTER the gate was read and BEFORE running; NON-CRITERION — they cannot reclassify the branch; instrument extension: a FEATURE-SUPPORT CLIP to the TRAIN [min, max] per feature, trivial-baseline rows and a REPORTED tel_vs_trivial in every run, and the equal-dims FREETOK arm = FREE ++ 2048-dim multi-hot of the anchor's top-32 next-token ids hashed mod 2048, deployment-free information from the same verify forward; the INVARIANCE LEG requires the extended tool at modes OFF to reproduce the registered numbers bit-exact). INVARIANCE LEG: holds on every gate-bearing field — all twelve deltas/CIs, the gate, per-population values, test MSEs, chosen lambdas, VAL tables, cosines, per-coordinate quartiles, and all 1,512 registered state rows are bit-identical; the REPORTED trivial MSE differs by ONE ULP (0.9900697257801255 registered vs 0.9900697257801256 extended; 1.1e-16 — the amendment accumulates trivial SSE per cluster before summing, the registered code kept a running scalar; the critic's own from-raws recompute had landed on ...259 for the same reason) and two of the three R^2 values (free, tel) inherit that last digit exactly (noise is bit-identical; both recompute bit-exact from the extended trivial). Explained per the pin; the diagnostics stand. The extended tool's tel_vs_trivial for the REGISTERED configuration reproduces the critic's number bit-exact: +0.10701294951277318 [-0.06983322605482828, +0.22087590283869973]; k3 +0.2163 [+0.2037, +0.2287]; oq15 -0.0002 [-0.3557, +0.2238]. Under the clip: TEST MSE free 0.9084 / tel 0.7393 / noise 0.9010 vs trivial 0.9901 -> R^2 +0.083 / +0.253 / +0.090; delta_noise +0.161692 [+0.153035, +0.170259] (stale points -0.000805 / -0.001043 / -0.000840 / -0.001285 / -0.000738 — the control tightens to sd 0.00022); delta_free +0.169079 [+0.160250, +0.177775]; gate label under the clip STAGE-R PASS (REPORTED — cannot reclassify); chosen lambdas free/tel 1e4 (INTERIOR, the first non-edge selections of the lane), noise 1e5 (edge); TEL centered-cosine median 0.502, per-coordinate R^2 quartiles [+0.208, +0.236, +0.271]. DESIGN LIMIT (critic blocker, wording corrected pre-commit): DR-2 compares TEL against FREETOK, not TEL++TOK against FREETOK, so it licenses "the telemetry margin is not evidence of information beyond what p already reveals at this readout" — NOT "the margin is composed of token identity" (FREETOK beats TEL in only 59/84 clusters; the two encodings could carry partly different information netting to a tie). WHAT THIS CORRECTS IN THE SECTIONS ABOVE AND BELOW: DR-2's licensed sentence ("not evidence of information beyond p") was the TEL-vs-FREETOK reading; DR-3 is the direct test — the S1 stream DOES carry block-aligned, dilution-priced information about the anchor state beyond FREETOK's 2048-bin hashed encoding of p's top-32 support, small in absolute terms (+0.065 standardized MSE) but CI-tight and replicating across two disjoint prompt populations. QUALIFIER (critic blocker): the anchor destroys all block-aligned slot information, so it cannot separate slot information that is a function of p's EXACT top-32 (identity lost to the ~62-ids-per-bin hash, value-to-id pairing lost) from information beyond p — "beyond p" is unpriced until an equal-dims better-token-encoding comparator (DR-4 candidate: FREE ++ TOK(hash A) ++ TOK(salted hash B) at 4753 dims, or a rank-paired TOK) is registered and run. The K-2 sentence stands with that qualifier: most of what telemetry reconstructs is a lossy copy of what p reveals; between a third and a half of its margin is not, depending on whether dilution is priced (union_vs_freetok / delta_free = 0.0652/0.1691 = 38.5% raw; union_vs_unionnoise / delta_free = 46% dilution-priced). WHAT IT DOES NOT LICENSE: any conversion-level claim — whether that R^2 increment (+0.066 of R^2; +0.065 of standardized MSE) buys acceptance at matched budget through an SD^2-style injection is Stage A's question, unmeasured, with two conversion nulls on this lane against it. Pinned magnitudes: 1.047 of the DR-3 margin (the surprising side (ii) fired mechanically and VACUOUSLY — the comparator is worse than FREETOK, so 'surviving' measures its loss) and -0.018 of the free margin. Honest prediction (teeth at +0.02..+0.05): FAILED ON SIGN, recorded against. CAVEAT (critic blocker; heuristic, not a registered readout): the teeth readout is UNANCHORED — no alignment-destroyed TOK_B twin was registered, so it charges the block its +2048-dim dilution; in the committed val_mse table FREETOK2 beats FREETOK at lambda 1e5 (0.77768 vs 0.79394) and the live-minus-stale-mean VAL gap is positive at every lambda (+0.047 at 1e-2 ... +0.031 at 1e4, +0.020 at 1e5), so the live -0.0031 is consistent with a block-aligned contribution of order +0.02..+0.03 offset by dilution of the same order at the selected lambda 1e4 — and equally with less. LICENSED: in a shared-lambda ridge TOK_B does not net a positive margin over FREETOK at either arm's VAL-optimal lambda; its block-aligned contribution is not separable from dilution under the registered readout. NOT licensed: 'extracts nothing', 'saturated', 'pure dilution' (the stale variants sit at lambda 1e5, where dilution is an order smaller). The anchored test (FREETOK ++ alignment-destroyed TOK_B) would resolve it but is a DR-5 under the fold-back-#2 hard stop — run only if the user authorizes an exception in those words. Dated erratum to the registration: TOK_B is sparse like TOK_A, with heavy-tailed values (median nonzero 0.022; 4.7% float16 underflow), not 'dense-valued'. The six DR-3 arms reproduce bit-identically (3108/3108 shared state rows); the -dirty meta is attested moot by the clean-worktree re-run. WHAT IT MEANS: DR-4 did NOT deliver the basis the registration said a Stage-A telemetry test NEEDS; 'beyond p' stays UNPRICED; the pre-registered recommendation stands (treat as NOT confirmed for the Stage-A decision) and Stage A, if run, proceeds on the user's decision with that on the table; FREETOK stays the Stage-A free comparator; a Stage-A telemetry WIN could only ever be licensed as 'converts beyond FREETOK, the strongest free encoding tested, at the actuator', never 'beyond p'.
- WHAT OQ-16 ACTUALLY ANSWERED (the ledger's literal question — "what fraction of SD^2's vector survives reconstruction from the address stream?"): linearly, about a quarter (R^2 ~0.25, median direction cosine ~0.50 in standardized space, with the support clip) — and a free encoding of the output distribution's top-32 token identities, which the same verify forward hands over anyway, recovers at least as much (CI-clear). At this readout the telemetry margin is therefore not evidence of information beyond what p already reveals; whether telemetry adds anything ON TOP of p is DR-3's question. (DATED NOTE 2026-09-04: DR-3 answered it — see the DR-3 ADDENDUM in the DR block above; this sentence, the "ties a free encoding of p" phrase, and the K-2 location below are SUPERSEDED in the addendum's qualified sense: the stream adds beyond FREETOK's hashed encoding of p, CI-clear; "beyond p" itself is unpriced pending DR-4.) Located against the kill criteria: this is K-2's "a lossy copy of information the decoder already has" at the reconstruction level; it does not re-fire K-2 (that verdict was on future-concept probes at offsets >= 2, F-15, a different target). This is the OQ-6 h-probe story from the other side: extracting FROM h under a cheap budget lost to telemetry (committed), and reconstructing TOWARD h from telemetry ties a free encoding of p. - FORMAL STATE + STAGE-A DECISION INPUTS: by the pinned partition, Stage R is a PASS (the registered FREE arm was probe_input's 657 dims; FREETOK was not a registered baseline and DR-2 is NON-CRITERION) and Stage A (SD^2-style draft-side injection of the reconstructed vector) is AUTHORIZED and USER-GATED; nothing runs from this finding, and the lane hard-stops after Stage A regardless of its outcome. HONEST WEIGHT for that decision, in the registration's own words: DR-2's TOKEN-IDENTITY-NOT-EXCLUDED label is carried into the Stage-A cost decision and mandates FREETOK (or a stronger token-identity encoding) as a free comparator in any actuator economics. With DR-3 in view (the addendum above): a Stage-A telemetry test now HAS a reconstruction-level basis — the UNION reconstruction is CI-clear better than the FREETOK one (R^2 +0.324 vs +0.258), so the Stage-A telemetry claim pinned for the freeze is UNION-vs-FREETOK at the actuator, both free-augmented (the STAGE-A FREEZE landed 2026-09-05 in docs/BLUEPRINT.md, pre-GPU, critic-gated twice: SD^2-style up-projection injection with a frozen drafter and a rank-512 zero-initialised adapter; arms vanilla / oracle_h (TRUE h_a, the actuator ceiling, D-2-named) / free_inject = FREETOK / tel_inject = UNION on the fresh 210-prompt data/prompts_oq16_verdict.jsonl population (mechanical paraphrase refusal against all four committed sets, 0 overlaps); ladder A0 (oracle vs vanilla at the unchanged E6 +0.062 bar) before A1 (the free-actuator readout F-vs-V and the telemetry verdict T-vs-V AND T-vs-F); each rung user-launched; DR-4's COMPARATOR-TOOTHLESS label and its pinned recommendation are carried into the user's A1 decision); a TEL-vs-FREETOK actuator test would have none (TEL is CI-clear slightly worse than FREETOK). Two conversion nulls on this lane (F-20/F-21) remain the closest precedents, and whether a +0.065 standardized-MSE increment in the reconstructed vector moves acceptance at matched budget is unmeasured — no probability is licensed either way. What Stage A could also measure is whether an SD^2-style injection of a FREE reconstruction converts at all (a different question, outside the K-3 kill text's telemetry claim, and not authorized by this partition without its own registration). Mandatory Stage-A freeze elements pinned by this finding: the support clip (or an sd floor) on every live feature — an actuator injecting a 5.76-MSE reconstruction on an out-of-support prompt is worse than nothing (critic A3); a lambda family containing the trivial predictor; the pairwise-selected-budget check (F-21); and — DR-2 having landed TOKEN-IDENTITY-NOT-EXCLUDED — the FREETOK arm (or a stronger token-identity encoding) as a mandatory free comparator in the actuator economics. What the pass says: a linear map from the address stream recovers the anchor state's direction at median cosine ~0.5 in standardized space and beats the free features in every test cluster; what it does not say: that the recovered subspace is the steering-relevant one (Stage A's question), that the margin survives raw-h-space injection (the standardized/raw distinction is pinned), or that any of this converts to tokens/call at matched budget (two nulls on this lane say the conversion bar is high).
- caveats: single model pair, single stream, NF4; linear reconstruction only (registered economy) — a nonlinear head is a different claim; float16 target; standardized-space cosine (raw-space injection differs by the per-coordinate 1/sd reweighting); the FREETOK encoding is coarse (~62 ids per bin) and lower-bounds what exact token identity could explain; no wall-clock claims (D-9); the registered numbers stand as registered — the DR runs are amendments to the deployable feature map and are never folded into them.

## F-23 — OQ-16 CLOSED BY DATED ERRATUM (TARGET-FREE): the anchor hidden state the reconstruction lane targeted is an exact linear function of the verifier's own next-token distribution — free information under D-5 — so the telemetry reconstruction is a lossy copy of what the decoder already has and the Stage-A telemetry actuator test is void before any GPU spend; the lane is closed with no partition branch claimed (limbo clause honored)
- date: 2026-09-05
- regime: MATHEMATICAL IDENTITY, numerically checked on REAL byproduct rows (Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct lm_head, untied and bias-free, read from the cached bf16 checkpoint as float32; 48 pinned rows = the first 4 blocks of the first 3 prompts of each of the four registered vanilla gamma-4 verdict runs, every npz sha-bound to its committed summary; CPU compute on the GPU box — the tool needs the cached weights and the gitignored raws; CI imports the module through tests/test_ha_identity.py, where torch and safetensors load lazily, and never runs it; the identity is generic to any linear unembedding of full column rank with vocab > d, tied or untied, and is measured here on one model). CLAIM: h_a (the final-norm output at the anchor, the registered Stage-R target) is determined by the anchor's logits, and therefore by the anchor's full next-token distribution p — the quantity D-5 names as the mandatory free baseline ("steering from the target's distribution at the last verified position, which every speculative decoder computes anyway"). W_U @ h reproduces the logged argmax 48/48 and the logged top-32 ids at 0.9948 mean overlap (min 0.969; max top-1 probability gap 0.0206 against the values logged by the live bf16 lm_head of the NF4-loaded verifier); the least-squares inverse from the float32 logits recovers h_a at median relative error 2.5e-8 (max 3.2e-8), the float32 rounding floor (from float64 logits 1.9e-14); closed-form least-squares recovery from only the top-k (id, log-prob) pairs of the recomputed logits (not the logged float16 pairs) reaches median per-row R^2 +1.0000 at k = 4097, +0.506 at 2048, +0.216 at 512, +0.078 at 128, +0.014 at 32 (median cosines 1.000 / 0.711 / 0.465 / 0.280 / 0.123) — a per-row R^2 about the row's own mean in raw h space from a prior-free solve, NOT Stage-R's standardized pooled-MSE R^2 and not comparable to the ridge arms' +0.258 / +0.324; the top-k recovery uses log-probabilities only with the log-partition as an extra unknown, so p alone, not the logits, determines h_a. CONDITIONING: W_U has full column rank (singular values 1.453 .. 94.5, condition 65); the all-ones vector, the softmax's free direction, lies 99.2% inside col(W_U), yet the augmented system [W_U, -1] has smallest singular value 1.451, so nothing is weakly determined; from bfloat16-rounded full log-probabilities with logZ unknown, h_a recovers to median relative error 3.5e-03 (max 6.9e-03). WHAT STANDS: every Stage-R and DR-1..DR-4 readout as pinned (F-22) — the gates were run against their registered arms and recomputed bit-exact. WHAT FALLS: their meaning for the lane. The registered FREE arm (657 anchor summaries; R^2 +0.083) and the DR-2/DR-4 token encodings (FREETOK +0.258, FREETOK2 +0.255) were lossy proxies for the free information D-5 names, from which the target follows exactly (R^2 1.000); the stream's reconstruction (TEL +0.253; UNION +0.324) is therefore K-2's "lossy copy of information the decoder already has", with no hashing caveat and no further comparator needed — "beyond p" is priced at this target: nothing is. The Stage-A telemetry arms (F = FREETOK reconstruction, T = UNION reconstruction) are struck pre-GPU: a host that computes p for rejection sampling can inject h_a itself with at most one least-squares solve against the cached lm_head (a 4096 x 128256 matvec plus a precomputed 4096 x 4096 inverse; this harness holds h_a directly through its final-norm catch). "Free" is D-5's convention; D-1's premise never priced shipping p or h_a to a disaggregated drafter (K-6 territory, not settled here). The oracle arm O (true h_a) is thereby the DEPLOYABLE free-actuator arm — SD^2-lite from the verifier's own distribution — whose V-vs-O readout sits outside the K-3 kill text and this lane's claim; it needs its own registration if run (label FREE-ACTUATOR WIN / NULL; a WIN is an SD^2 configuration result, not a sideband result). WHAT IT DOES NOT TOUCH, AND WHY: the existence results F-15/F-17 concern future-position concepts at offsets 2-3, which the anchor's p does not determine; but their free arm (a) is the same 657-summary proxy, so "beyond p?" arises there too, and the record's answer is the OQ-6 h-probe row of the same grids (margins_h_vs_a in results/real/probes_s1{,_replication}.json and probes_s3{,_replication}.json): under the identity a linear probe on h_a is a linear probe on the full logit vector, the strongest linear free-p baseline, and at the pinned L1 offset-2 cells it adds nothing over (a) — h-a margin S1 +0.0009 [-0.0087, +0.0107] primary, -0.0012 [-0.0112, +0.0090] replication; S3 -0.0026 [-0.0071, +0.0021], -0.0042 [-0.0093, +0.0015] — against c-a margins +0.0163 / +0.0141 (S1) and +0.0147 / +0.0173 (S3), and sits below (a) at offsets 3-4 on all four grids (offset-3 h-a -0.0205 / -0.0399 / -0.0111 / -0.0173). The K-2 margins are therefore not linear-logit information and F-15/F-17 stand; a NONLINEAR-in-p free baseline at the registered mlp256 capacity was never run and is the unrun K-2 comparator (not authorized here). The topic controls F-16/F-18 and the conversion nulls F-19/F-21 stand on their own. The only reconstruction target where telemetry could carry non-free information is h at FUTURE anchors (OQ-16's own anticipatory variant, never run); the fold-back-#2 hard stop forbids a third arc without the user's explicit exception. CLASSIFICATION: no partition branch is claimed. The limbo clause's condition is met (a Stage A never run after a PASS) and its rule, "neither limbo may ever be re-labeled a null", is honored; the catch-all is not invoked because the branch it would name (RECONSTRUCTION-CEILING NULL, "Stage-R leg 1 fails") is contradicted by the measured leg-1 pass; the disposition is the partition's own VOID precedent (no branch claimed, a dated note, the lane stops in practice under the fold-back-#2 hard stop). The lane is CLOSED BY DATED ERRATUM, mechanism TARGET-FREE; STAGE-R PASS stands as the mechanical outcome of its gate; the DR-3 OUTCOME's "reopens the Stage-A telemetry question" sentence is superseded. The lane ENDS. DISCLOSURE: the target was chosen for logging convenience (model.norm, one hook) rather than SD^2's mid-layer read (layers 3, L/2, L-2), which no identity of this kind ties to p (many-to-one; how much of those states p predicts is unmeasured); the free arm was built from the existing probe_input summaries rather than from D-5's definition; three diagnostics priced "beyond FREETOK's encoding" while the closed-form free comparator was one linear solve away. The observation came from the 2026-09-05 creative-lens adversarial pass over the gathered data (contrarian lens), reproduced by that pass's verifier, by an operator scratch check and by the committed tool; CRITIC RECOMPUTE ROUND (2026-09-05, before this commit): an independent implementation (float64 QR inverse; SVD least squares for the top-k solves; log-probabilities only) reproduced the forward numbers exactly, the full-logit inverse to 8 significant figures and every k < 4097 number to 7 or more digits; it exposed the first draft's k = 4097 row (median +0.9999, min 0.9988) as an artifact of an ill-conditioned normal-equations solve with a 1e-8 ridge, replaced by the committed SVD solve, and confirmed full column rank and the augmented-system conditioning above; it also blocked three sentences of the first draft — a "mechanical" catch-all classification against the limbo clause, an unargued "does not touch F-15/F-17", and a "standardized R^2" label — all rewritten before the commit. ATTESTATION: results/real/oq16_ha_identity.json carries git_commit 8542ef5-dirty; at run time the tracked dirt was this commit's own documentation and registration text, the tool and its test were untracked, and every input is sha-bound (lm_head shard sha256 and the twelve npz sha256s in the file).

- DATED NOTE 2026-10-18 (prior art for the identity; the cold read of that date, docs/PUBLIC_CUT_PLAN.md 15.8): the identity this erratum rests on, that the final-norm state is the lm_head input, so the full logits are its image under the lm_head matrix and, that matrix having full column rank (the check this erratum's payload records), the state follows from them, is the setting of the published softmax-bottleneck work: Finlayson, Ren and Swayamdipta, "Logits of API-Protected LLMs Leak Proprietary Information" (arXiv 2403.09539, 2024), which shows the logits confined to a linear subspace of dimension equal to the hidden size, and Carlini et al., "Stealing Part of a Production Language Model" (arXiv 2403.06634, 2024), which recovers the embedding projection layer up to symmetries from API access; Finlayson, Ren and Swayamdipta do not claim recovery of the state, and Carlini et al. recover the projection rather than a state; the state's recovery follows from the identity here. Both fetched on 2026-10-18 and added to docs/PRIOR_ART.md (its 2026-10-18 additions). The record already concedes the order of events in docs/IDEAS.md line 247 ("textbook prior art (Finlayson 2024, Carlini 2024) that nobody in the lane applied": Stage R ran and Stage A was frozen before the identity was applied). This note places that concession against the literature; the erratum's text stands as written. [withheld: docs/IDEAS.md, docs/PUBLIC_CUT_PLAN.md not in this repository; see WITHHELD.md]

(iii) THE TWIN WAS NOT INERT, AND THE CORRECTED TWIN READ: the S1 critic recompute found what the registration had not anticipated — the shift twin reads a position 17 tokens ahead of the anchor, whose forward pass has consumed the label token (the offset-2 label sits at a+1) for 93.7% (S1) / 93.5% (S3) of anchors, and it gains CI-clear over the base on both primary gates (Mn above), about as much as the aligned block, so D = Mt - Mn sits at zero by subtraction; F-16 had named that channel only as one that "could only make M2n an UNDERestimate", which is true under SURVIVES and runs toward the null under a kill. At S3 the dependence is confined to the primary harvest (the replication's Mn and both its Mt uppers sit below the floor); at S1 the ABSORBED-versus-NOT-SHOWN call on the replication rides on D specifically (with an inert twin D would equal Mt, whose upper +0.0052 is above the floor). TWIN-ROBUST and every D of this entry stand: the largest D upper under the three approximations is +0.0042 / +0.0039 / +0.0047, below the +0.005 floor. On the primary the corrected twin itself gains CI-clear over the base while T's CI includes zero, so the label-consumption mechanism is WITHDRAWN for that harvest: the shift twin's gain is matched by a prompt-independent block, which under the pinned reading means a same-marginal sparse block of these dims yields about Mt at this reader — the strongest form of the null, under which Mt stops being evidence of telemetry information (the registered reading of one construction, not a measurement of every block); D_r remains the matched comparison. On the replication the mechanism is neither withdrawn nor supported (Mn_r and T straddle zero). NET AT THIS READER, a gate-cell (mlp256) statement: the three sparse blocks measured — the aligned telemetry, the same row's telemetry 17 positions ahead, and a different prompt's telemetry at the same anchor index — add +0.0005..+0.0034 over the full free baseline (Mt / Mn / Mn_r: CI-clear for all three on both primaries, on the S1 replication for the aligned block and, by +0.0002, the prompt-level twin, and for none on the S3 replication), and the aligned block carries no MATERIAL margin beyond either twin at this power (D and D_r points +0.0000..+0.0013 with every upper at or below +0.0029; the S1 replication's D and D_r lowers are -0.0003 and -0.0002, so "nothing" is not licensed); a helpful twin lowers D_r below Mt by the identity D_r = Mt - Mn_r, which is the matched comparison, not dilution. The pinned reading extends the one prompt-independent construction to any same-marginal sparse block of these dims — a registered reading, not a measurement. The S1 replication's helps trigger is CI-clear by +0.0002 only, and its seed-0 cell reproduces the disclosed second smoke cell bit-exactly (a fresh training under the fresh-state pin; the F-18 accidental determinism check again — so the 0.0027 gap between the two smoke cells was the construction change, not CUDA jitter, a dated note on the block's SMOKE CONTACT sentence). Not re-examined at the corrected twin, which ran the gate cell only: the S1 offset-3 rows that carry OFF3-ABSORBED (the shift twin is CI-clear positive there, Mn off3 +0.0015 [+0.0005, +0.0025] / +0.0019 [+0.0000, +0.0038]), the S1 linear rows (replication Mt linear +0.0032 [+0.0010, +0.0055], upper above the floor, ABSORBED riding on D linear +0.0012 [-0.0002, +0.0026]) and the S3 primary linear row — those legs rest on the registered twin only. At linear capacity none of the blocks adds (S3 primary Mt linear +0.0003 [-0.0009, +0.0016], Mn linear -0.0012 [-0.0027, +0.0003]; S1 primary Mt linear -0.0002 [-0.0017, +0.0014]), so the +0.003 is mlp256-specific, consistent with a dims-or-optimization effect rather than telemetry content. The per-cluster correlations recorded in the twin payloads (aligned gain against twin gain, +0.58..+0.76) are a shared-subtrahend artifact — the three deltas share the fplus probe — and license nothing: cross-seed the correlations collapse to about +0.1 and a same-subtrahend control sits at +0.49 (critic-recomputed on the S3 primary; no committed artifact records it); the twin block's descriptive "shared row-level content" reading of them is withdrawn by dated note. The twin diagnostic's own pinned prediction FAILED in every part: branch (TWIN-ROBUST at both sources, predicted S1 TWIN-DEPENDENT), Mn_r sign (+0.0032 / +0.0033 CI-clear on the primaries, predicted near zero or slightly negative) and T (about zero everywhere, predicted +0.002..+0.003 on the S1 primary) — ROBUST arrived through a non-inert twin, not through the near-inert premise the block allowed for; the future-context mechanism the diagnostic was registered to price is withdrawn under its own pinned reading where the corrected twin helps CI-clear (both S1 harvests, the S3 primary), and is neither withdrawn nor supported on the S3 replication, where Mn, Mn_r and T all straddle zero. A weak free channel both twins share — the anchor's position — was measured by the critic on both primaries and is negligible (a position-only predictor gains nothing over the floor; no committed artifact records it). The S3 primary linear D row keeps its CI-positive-immaterial qualifier. (iv) THE READER: best VAL epochs on the h_a-bearing arms were 0-2 at every gate cell on every harvest (S1 primary 0 / 2 / 1 for all three arms; S1 replication 2 / 0 / 1; S3 primary fplus 1 1 1, fplus_b 1 0 1, fplus_n 1 1 1; S3 replication fplus 0 1 0, fplus_b 1 1 1, fplus_n 0 1 1; every S1 off3 cell 0) against 9-24 for the committed (a) mlp256 cells and 2-11 for (b)/(c) — the effective reader on these arms is one to three AdamW passes, a fact the registration disclosed from the smoke and both labels are scoped to. The dense standardized hz block dominates the input norm (per-anchor L2 about 65 against at most about 11 for the 128-hot slots block and about 6 for TOK on S1; critic-recomputed, no committed artifact records it), the one mechanism by which this reader could return a null a stronger reader would not — the stronger-reader residual, named under both labels as registered. (v) THE PREDICTION FAILED ON BOTH SOURCES: the pinned honest prediction was S1 SURVIVES-FULL-FREE with D +0.005..+0.012 and Mt positive but smaller than D, S3 SPLIT or NOT-SHOWN with ABSORBED "unlikely at this power". S1 read ABSORBED, the registered "what would surprise" branch; Mt exceeded D on every harvest; and S3's ABSORBED arrived because the D CIs are half the width of the Phase-3 (b)-(a) CIs (the twins share 82-86% of their dims plus the seeded init and batch order) and because the non-inert twin pushed D to zero by subtraction — not a power story. The registered parenthetical for the S1 surprise ("the S1 margin was nonlinear-free information all along") is quoted as pre-registered text, not asserted: the reader-handicap reading is equally consistent with the data. CONSEQUENCES EXECUTED (dated, per the pinned rules; never rewrites): F-15 and F-17 receive dated errata scoped to the re-tested cells (S1 L1 offsets 2-3; S3 L1 offset 2 — L2 / L3 and the further offsets stand against the 657-summary proxy and are untested against the full free baseline); K-2 receives a dated STATUS line per source; F-16 and F-18 receive dated "moot" notes. ABSORBED at BOTH sources, twin-robust at both: the existence layer is NOT SHOWN anywhere at the registered reader; K-2's 2026-09-05 STATUS salvage line ("the negative results and the method", docs/WHY_THIS_DIES.md K-2 STATUS 2026-09-05) is all that stands; scorecards get dated STATUS lines, never rewrites.
- caveats: Every label is a statement about the registered reader at the registered budget on the pinned L1 cells — one to three AdamW passes over a dense standardized block plus a sparse one — and a stronger or differently balanced reader is a named residual under ABSORBED exactly as it would have been under SURVIVES; one reader cannot establish K-2's universal "never beat". "ABSORBED" is the registered strong-null form (every D upper below +0.005 on both harvests), not a demonstration that the equal-dims margin is zero: Mt is CI-clear positive at three of four gates, the S3 primary linear D row is CI-positive-immaterial, and the dilution identity D = Mt - Mn means the label depended on the shift twin's own gain at the S1 replication and the S3 primary — the corrected-twin diagnostic prices that dependence and its reading is carried in (iii) with the pinned qualifier, as a post-hoc, non-criterion result. The full free baseline of this test is (a) ++ hashed top-32 ids ++ z-scored h_a; F-23's identity makes h_a an exact linear function of the full p_a, so "full" means D-5's free information as this pipeline can encode it, read by this reader — not every function of p. Mc's linear rows and the S3 replication's non-CI-clear Mt are descriptive; no consequence attaches to Mc. Leak-rider sensitivity: on S1 the shuffled fplus sits 1.5-1.9 points below the committed shuffled (a), so leg 1's effective threshold there is about 0.026 rather than 0.01 (the CI anchor-coordinate tests are the primary guard for the shared blocks); on S3 the slack is nominal (+0.0108 / +0.0107). Multiplicity: four uncorrected gate cells, one readout each, as registered. The S3 replication's -dirty attestation is documentation-only and critic-verified, not recorded by an artifact. Populations, prompts, models and tap designs are those of F-15 / F-17 (30 test prompt clusters; short factual prompts; one model per source; S2 never run). No conversion, acceptance or wall-clock claim; K-3's two nulls (F-19, F-21) stand untouched, as does the OQ-16 closure (F-23).

- DATED NOTE 2026-10-18 (the first free baseline and the routing literature; the cold read of that date, docs/PUBLIC_CUT_PLAN.md 15.8): the free baseline of F-15 and F-17 left token ids out "as non-ordinal" (tools/run_probes.py line 196, a frozen instrument). At S3, where the stream is OLMoE's router address stream, the absorption this finding measured once hashed predicted-token ids (TOK) and the z-scored h_a block entered the baseline together is consistent with two published analyses of MoE routing: OpenMoE (Xue et al., arXiv 2402.01739, 2024) reports routing decisions that depend mainly on the routed input token's ID with little regard to context (TOK holds predicted ids, so of the two only OLMoE's output-id sentence bears on TOK directly), and OLMoE (Muennighoff et al., arXiv 2409.02060, 2024), the paper of the base model behind S3's checkpoint (OLMoE-1B-7B-0125-Instruct, docs/METHOD.md line 269; the paper's section 5.4 analyses OLMoE-1B-7B), reports vocabulary specialization of its experts, later layers specializing on predicted output token IDs. Consistent with, not predicted by: the re-test added TOK and the standardized hz block in one arm, so the share of the absorption due to token ids alone was not isolated, and at S1 the stream is top-32-of-512 weight-slot occupancy at the down_proj inputs of a dense Llama (docs/GLOSSARY.md, the telemetry-sources entry), where the routing literature does not apply. Both papers fetched on 2026-10-18 and added to docs/PRIOR_ART.md. Categorical features are routinely one-hot or hash encoded; the exclusion was a design choice the re-test revisited, and the labels stand as written. The probing-practice references added the same day (Pimentel et al. 2020; Hewitt and Liang 2019; Xu et al. 2020) bear on the readers: untuned, scored on top-1 accuracy, and at F-15 and F-17 on raw inputs (this finding's reader reads z-scored h_a); the K-2 word is scoped to the registered reader throughout, and those references name what a stronger reader would be. [withheld: docs/PUBLIC_CUT_PLAN.md not in this repository; see WITHHELD.md]

At S1 L3, the cell the reroute was written around, the amended pass is NOT a CI-clear null: dE_br +0.0026 has upper +0.0054, at or above the +0.005 materiality floor, the only cell of eight where that holds; it passes because its lower bound -0.0004 is below zero. The diagnostic's pooled seeds 0-2 at that cell read dE_br +0.0018 [-0.0004, +0.0039] (upper below the floor, lower below zero): a power statement, not an exclusion. The registered raw leg is above its 0.01 band at TWO cells: S1 L3 (+0.0132, the fire that stopped the registered run) and S1_r L2 (+0.0105). COUNTERFACTUAL, stated as one: the frozen instrument was never run on the S1 replication; its grid trains a space's shuffled cells first and its rider raises SystemExit on a leg-2 fail before that space's first unshuffled cell, and the retrained shuffled rows in the committed state files reproduce the frozen runs' accuracies exactly (all 18 retrained rows across S1, S3 and S3_r equal their frozen rows' test_acc, max abs diff 0.0; S1 L3 seed 0 dE_br +0.0026 = the diagnostic's), so under the frozen rider the S1 replication WOULD HAVE halted at L2 and OQ-18 would have had NO S1 label. The amended leg at S1_r L2 reads dE_br -0.0005 [-0.0011, +0.0002] with the aligned arm's own dependence excess over fplus -0.0009: raw up, dependence down — prediction-marginal drift, one of the two non-leak mechanisms the amendment named (the S1 L3 fire itself read TWIN-MATCHED); this is the one place the reroute is exercised as a FRESH test, and the registered statistic fired there on drift alone. DIRECTION OF THE REROUTE, for the record: the registered instrument would have read no S1 label; the amendment enabled reading a kill-extension (S1 L2 ABSORBED, K-2 FIRED at L2) and a NOT-SHOWN, not a survival. RUNS: S1 primary resumed from the fired run's committed state file (resumed_cells 30, retrained_shuffled_rows 6); S1 replication fresh (resumed_cells 0); S3 primary and replication resumed from their frozen-instrument runs (resumed_cells 69 / 69, retrained_shuffled_rows 6 / 6) with the frozen-instrument payloads preserved, NEVER concluded, as results/real/fullfree_coarse_s3.rider_v1.json (sha 8fc04f277c72...) and fullfree_coarse_s3_r.rider_v1.json (sha 33e73c080f94...). Every L3 statement is read through the lopsided group lens named in the registration. The L1 verdict (F-24) is untouched; conversion (K-3), acceptance and wall-clock are untouched; S2 is not run. Mt and Mn_r are quoted beside every D because D = Mt - Mn_r by the paired identity; the corrected twin is label-inert by construction and not accuracy-inert (F-24). ## F-26 — OQ-9 STAGE 1 (the prefetch half; NON-CRITERION, a K-6 datum): on the OLMoE S3 harvests the router address stream is a prefetch source with headroom over the recency cache and a material margin over the free INPUT TOKEN ALONE (a one-token train table; the equally free previous token is not in the baseline and gate 0 routes on it, so the equal-dims free-bigram twin is the unrun next rung) at B = 16 of 64 — stage label PREFETCH-HEADROOM on the primary harvest, the seed replication agrees on every label; ADDRESS-BEYOND-TOKEN +0.1032 / +0.1072 at B = 16 and below the +0.05 floor at B = 32; INTRA-FORWARD +0.4861 / +0.4816; DRAFT (perfect-draft ceiling, horizon 2) +0.1556 / +0.1545
- date: 2026-09-15
- regime: REAL MODELS (CPU-only readouts on the two committed S3 harvests of allenai/OLMoE-1B-7B-0125-Instruct, NF4 double-quant with bf16 routers, target-only sampled generations at temperature 1.0, 256 new tokens, the 210 committed short prompts; primary seeds 0..7 (results/real/harvest_moe), replication seeds 8..11 (harvest_moe_r) on the SAME prompts; the sorted top-8 expert ids at the 16 router gates, no router weights; no model forward, no acceptance, no wall clock). Registered 2026-09-14 in docs/BLUEPRINT.md "OQ-9 STAGE-1 PREFETCH READOUTS REGISTRATION" before any readout ran on either harvest, critic-gated through three design passes and a final confirmation, frozen at c4c4511; NON-CRITERION for every existing finding (no K-2 / K-3 verdict, no F-15 / F-17 / F-24 / F-25 sentence and no existence or conversion label is touched); a systems datum for the K-6 memo. ESTIMAND: HIT RATE at residency budget B in {8, 16, 32} of the 64 experts per gate — the number of a target set's eight experts inside the resident set, summed over positions, divided by 8 times the positions; a COUNT, never bytes or latency; B = 16 is the label budget, 8 and 32 are reported. Readers fit on the train prompts (prompt_idx % 7 in {0..4}) pooled over seeds, lambda selected on val (5) from the widened grid {0.1, 1, 10, 100, 1000, 1e4, 1e5}, every number read on the 30 TEST prompts (6) pooled over seeds and positions with prompt-cluster bootstrap CI95 (n_boot 2000, seed 0); paired deltas on identical positions. ARMS per horizon j (S[t] the routing of the forward whose softmax is p_t; tokens[t] sampled from p_t is the FREE bonus token and feeds the forward whose routing is S[t+1] — the convention verified against the harvester pre-freeze and confirmed on the data by the critic's val-only diagnostic at landing): RECENCY_B = the B most recently used experts per gate (the cache policy); MARGINAL_B; FREE_TABLE = zero-fit top-B of TABLE(tokens[t]), the train token-conditional routing frequencies of the forward a token feeds (leave-one-prompt-out at train rows); FREE_TOK = ridge on TABLE(tokens[t]) — a ONE-TOKEN free arm, the D-5 analogue for prefetch as registered; HISTORY = ridge on the S[t] multi-hot, address-only; HISTORY_TOK = S[t] ++ TABLE(tokens[t]); at j = 2 only DRAFT_FREE / DRAFT_FULL add TABLE(tokens[t+1]), the TRUE next token as the D-2-named ceiling draft; INTRA = gate l from gates 0..l-k of the SAME forward (leads 1, 2, 4) against MARGINAL; (iv) a front-loading curve reported only. Readings SIGNAL iff paired CI95 lower > 0 and point >= +0.05 (the pinned materiality floor), NEGATIVE iff CI95 upper < 0, else NOT-SHOWN; HISTORY = HISTORY minus RECENCY_16 (j = 1), ADDRESS-BEYOND-TOKEN = HISTORY_TOK minus FREE_TOK (j = 1), INTRA-FORWARD = INTRA lead 1 minus MARGINAL_16 over target gates 1..15, DRAFT = DRAFT_FULL minus HISTORY_TOK (j = 2); CACHE-SUFFICIENT iff RECENCY_16 at j = 1 >= 0.90; the sideband sentence follows ADDRESS-BEYOND-TOKEN alone. Every label is scoped to the registered linear readers on the sorted top-8 id stream at hit rate at the registered budgets.
- source: results/real/prefetch_s3.json, prefetch_s3.json.state.jsonl, prefetch_s3.meta.json (primary, the VERDICT harvest); results/real/prefetch_s3_r.json, prefetch_s3_r.json.state.jsonl, prefetch_s3_r.meta.json (replication, confirmatory) — the state files sha-header bound to the shard shas recorded in the committed shard metas under results/real/harvest_moe (moe_s0..s7) and results/real/harvest_moe_r (moe_s8..s11); the .npz shards themselves are gitignored and tests/test_prefetch_landing.py checks the header against those metas; committed with this finding; tools/run_prefetch_readouts.py (the frozen instrument, sha 8529532d4d78..., numpy only, --conclude-from the exact recompute surface); tests/test_prefetch_readouts.py; tests/test_prefetch_landing.py (the committed payloads reproduce their own conclusion; the instrument on disk is the frozen one); docs/BLUEPRINT.md registration block with its PRE-FREEZE ROUND (four disclosed smokes on replication shard s8's val prompts, every one recomputed from its state file) and OUTCOME paragraph (the critic's landing recompute and its val-only alignment diagnostic are described there; the diagnostic's outputs are scratch, not evidence).
- finding: MECHANICAL LABELS from the primary harvest's TEST rows at B = 16, the replication computed identically (primary / replication): STAGE LABEL PREFETCH-HEADROOM, replication AGREES. CACHE-SUFFICIENT did not fire: RECENCY_16 at j = 1 0.5197 [0.5144, 0.5258] / 0.5155 [0.5092, 0.5223]; RECENCY_32 (P1's window) 0.7755 [0.7713, 0.7799] / 0.7750 [0.7701, 0.7802] — the P1-window kill would not have fired at its own budget. HISTORY SIGNAL +0.2867 [+0.2784, +0.2939] / +0.2901 [+0.2808, +0.2978]. ADDRESS-BEYOND-TOKEN SIGNAL +0.1032 [+0.0926, +0.1146] / +0.1072 [+0.0952, +0.1206], positive and at or above the floor on 30 of 30 test prompts on both harvests (per-prompt minimum +0.0683 / +0.0699); the sideband sentence in its pinned words: "the address stream adds a material margin over the free input token at this reader". SCOPE OF THAT SENTENCE (the critic's one blocking objection, on the interpretation, not on the mechanical label; carried on every text of this landing): "the free input token" is a ONE-TOKEN train table of tokens[t]. The previous token tokens[t-1] is exactly as free to the decoder and is not in the baseline, and the data say gate 0 of OLMoE routes primarily on it: the payload's per-gate ADDRESS-BEYOND-TOKEN is largest at gate 0 (+0.221 / +0.224, differences of the payload's per-gate points, no per-gate bootstrap exists; free_tok 0.676 / 0.672 is its weakest gate there, and history 0.834 / 0.834 has its largest lead over free_tok at any gate there, +0.158 / +0.162), and the critic's UNREGISTERED val-only diagnostic (zero-fit top-16 tables keyed on tokens[t+s] against S[t+1] on identical val positions, train-fit, test never touched; scratchpad only, NOT evidence) reads at gate 0 0.813 / 0.798 for the previous token against 0.672 / 0.656 for the current one. So an unknown, plausibly large share of the +0.10 is FREE token-history information that a one-token table cannot encode, not address-stream information; the smoke trend (+0.180, +0.156, +0.148 as the free arm was strengthened, then +0.103 on the full-coverage primary, where the last step is coverage, a 0.519 to 0.055 fallback share on over a hundred times the train positions, not a reader change) points the same way. Nothing in this landing reads ADDRESS-BEYOND-TOKEN as context information in the address stream beyond free information or as support for the sideband premise; the next rung, NOT run and NOT registered here, is the equal-dims free-bigram twin TABLE(tokens[t]) ++ TABLE(tokens[t-1]) ++ bias against HISTORY_TOK at B = 16, j = 1 (one horizon-1 Gram on the primary, about 25 CPU minutes), registered with its prediction pinned before the read — the critic's: the margin drops to or below the +0.05 floor, driven by gate 0. INTRA-FORWARD SIGNAL +0.4861 [+0.4710, +0.5009] / +0.4816 [+0.4663, +0.4975]. PROVENANCE: both payloads record git c4c4511 with an empty tracked-status list, instrument sha 8529532d4d78..., smoke false, the full seed sets, 30 test prompts, 39,330 state rows each; primary 294,146 / 58,985 / 59,525 train / val / test positions at j = 1, 1453.5 s of instrument CPU time; replication 148,276 / 29,030 / 29,945, 791.5 s; --conclude-from reproduced both payloads exactly. One BLOCKING objection, on the interpretation of ADDRESS-BEYOND-TOKEN, applied as the SCOPE sentence beside the sideband sentence in every text. Seven advisories applied: the budget dependence of the labeled margin and of the draft twin reported beside the label; the table-sparsity bound; the two magnitude sub-clauses scored MISSED-HIGH rather than rounded to held; (iv) read with FREE_PREV as a near-majority classifier, GATES<=1 at offset 2 as the exact majority classifier, the floor named as the TRAIN majority class at its TEST rate, the class as the merged glue class, and offset 2 not monotone; the lambda constraint on the joint reader and the replication's grid edge; the replication named a SEED replication; runtime kept away from any latency sentence.
- post-hoc reading (2026-09-15; written AFTER the labels were read; NOT a registered readout; changes no label, licenses nothing): (1) the free one-token table alone ties the address-only reader at B = 16 (0.8111 vs 0.8064; 0.8036 vs 0.8056) and beats it at B = 8 (0.6171 vs 0.5942; 0.6102 vs 0.5927): whatever HISTORY_TOK adds over FREE_TOK arrives only in combination with the token, never in place of it. (2) The token's margin over the cache decays with horizon (FREE_TOK minus RECENCY_16 +0.2913 at j = 1 to +0.0427 at j = 4) while ADDRESS-BEYOND-TOKEN stays near +0.10 at every horizon (+0.1032 to +0.0933) and HISTORY_TOK minus HISTORY falls from +0.1079 to +0.0150: the token informs the forward it feeds, the routing history informs the horizon — where, under the SCOPE above, "routing history" may be standing in for free token history. (3) Per target gate at lead 1 the intra-forward reader (gate l from gates 0..l-1 of the same forward) rises from 0.668 at gate 1 to 0.961-0.978 at gates 8-15 (replication 0.672 to 0.961-0.979; not monotone in the tail): each gate l >= 8 is predictable from the gates before it at hit rate above 0.96 at B = 16 — the registration's "timing cell", in the prior art's own cell and unpriced (D-9); no reader from gates 0..7 alone was run. (4) Per gate RECENCY_16 ranges 0.40-0.64 and HISTORY_TOK 0.88-0.94 (replication 0.40-0.63, 0.87-0.94).
- caveats: A hit-rate count at linear readers on a harvest: no bytes, latency, wall-clock or boundary is priced, so K-5 / K-6 stay UNTESTED (D-9) and no speedup sentence follows; the runtimes above are instrument CPU time. The registered free arm is a ONE-TOKEN table; the equally free previous token is not in it, and the gate-0 datum says the labeled ADDRESS-BEYOND-TOKEN margin is partly free token-history information of unknown share — the equal-dims free-bigram twin is the unrun, unregistered next rung, and until it runs this finding says nothing about context information in the address stream beyond free information (the K-2 shape one level up). The prior-art cells are OCCUPIED as pinned (address-only history tables, cross-layer routing predictors, draft-conditioned prefetch; ids to verify before any external citation); the value is the measurement on this model with the record's own free baseline. ADDRESS-BEYOND-TOKEN shrinks with budget (+0.13 at B = 8, +0.10 at B = 16, +0.04 at B = 32 where the free token table already covers 0.93): whether it is worth anything is a cost question this stage does not pose. The DRAFT reading is a ceiling: the "draft" is the true next token (D-2), no drafter is paired with OLMoE. The readers are linear on the sorted top-8 id stream without router weights; a stronger reader or the weights are named residuals under every label. The replication is a SEED replication on the same 30 test and 150 train prompts: "agrees" licenses seed-level, not prompt-level, generalisation. (iv) is a glue-dominated top-1 accuracy (the floor class holds 58-59% of test positions) and reported only. The steer half of OQ-9 stays PARKED (no actuator in the record converts). Multiplicity: four label readings and three budgets, uncorrected, the replication as the insurance. A clean-worktree rerun is expected to reproduce the state files to the integer but is not guaranteed bit-exact under a different BLAS configuration; --conclude-from is the exact recompute surface.
