PINNED SENTENCE: "the address block adds a material margin over an equal-dims free bigram block and the address stream adds a material margin over the free bigram at this reader (B = 16, j = 1)". IN THE PINNED WORDS: the address block adds +0.0609 [+0.0514, +0.0712] over an equal-dims free bigram block and +0.0661 [+0.0569, +0.0762] over the free bigram at this reader; the critic's bigram objection is answered at THIS ENCODING (two additive 1024-dim train tables), not for previous-token information in general. COVERAGE CAVEAT (a caveat on the interpretation; the mechanical label is not contested): on the test bigram positions the current-token key has no train count at a share 0.0549 (previous-token key 0.0551; either 0.0987; both 0.0113; token-only counts). F-26's HARD BOUND (D_eq - f) / (1 - f), which needs only that D_eq at a fallback position is at most 1, gives the D_eq on positions where BOTH keys are covered at least -0.0419 here (f the either-unseen share; with the current-token share alone, +0.0063), so the bound does NOT establish a material margin on covered keys. The critic's UNREGISTERED val-only stratified diagnostic at this landing (readers re-fit exactly as the instrument fits them and read at the payload's selected lambdas on the VAL bigram positions; pooled val reproduces the payload's val table to six decimals; test rows not read; re-run by the assistant with identical output; scratch only, NOT evidence) reads D_eq +0.0454 where both keys have a train count (0.9115 of val) — BELOW the floor — against +0.1844 where the current token is unseen, +0.1190 where the previous token is unseen and +0.4491 where both are; D_add +0.0511 on the covered positions. On the TEST rows (state-row arithmetic joined to token-only coverage): per-prompt D_eq tracks the per-prompt fallback share (r 0.787 with the current-token share, 0.817 with either key unseen); the fifteen prompts at or below the median share pool to D_eq +0.0429 against +0.0793 above it; D_eq is at or above the floor on 16 of 30 prompts (positive on 30; range +0.0313..+0.1431; F-26's margin cleared the floor on 30 of 30). READING: the material part of the surviving pooled margin sits where the free tables are blind — positions whose token key the train tables never saw: every fallback stratum on val reads D_eq +0.1190..+0.4491 against +0.0454 on covered positions. The diagnostic LOCATES the margin; it does not identify the mechanism. At unseen PREVIOUS tokens the location is consistent with the registered identity qualification (S[t] is the routing of the forward tokens[t-1] fed); at unseen CURRENT tokens — the largest stratum — S[t] was computed before tokens[t] was sampled and cannot expose that token's identity: it can carry only the forward's PREDICTION of the token (information the output distribution p_t holds, free under D-5; the harvest's stored mass16 / mass64 / entropy summaries of p_t were never in the prefetch free baseline — stage 1 read them only in the reported-only front-loading arm) or context. SURVIVES therefore holds at THIS ENCODING on the pooled positions as registered, and F-26's sentence 'nothing in this landing reads ADDRESS-BEYOND-TOKEN as context information in the address stream beyond free information or as support for the sideband premise' STANDS unchanged. NEXT RUNG (unrun, unregistered; this CORRECTS the rung the registration named): the STRATIFIED covered-against-fallback read of D_eq on the TEST split at the selected lambdas, with a free arm built from the harvest's mass16 / mass64 / entropy beside it (the critic's cheapest experiment: Bigram.fit plus a per-position mask, about 75 / 45 s of elapsed time on val) — its branches are pinned at that rung's own registration, not here; in outline, covered-position D_eq SIGNAL on the primary would retire this caveat and NOT-SHOWN would make its materiality a coverage effect at this encoding; the pair-keyed (true bigram) table named at registration has WORSE coverage and would move D_eq by this very location effect, so it is not the next rung; a longer free history (tokens[t-2]) shares the problem unless its encoding handles unseen keys; on the replication the same diagnostic reads covered-position D_eq +0.0472 (0.8634 of val), the hard bound -0.0128, r 0.804, the floor cleared on 17 of 30 prompts. TWIN-EXEMPTION ANCHORS: free_all - FREE_BIGRAM +0.0017 [+0.0015, +0.0020] and HISTORY_BIGRAM - history_tok +0.0052 [+0.0049, +0.0054], CI-clear positive — above the priced range's upper end (-0.0045..+3e-5) by about two orders of magnitude, one order below the floor rather than two (F-24's corrected twin helped the same way); the readings are unaffected (D_add - D_eq equals the second anchor exactly), the price was wrong; replication +0.0016 [+0.0014, +0.0019] and +0.0046 [+0.0043, +0.0049]. NON-CRITERION: the mechanical labels of this finding (stage label, four readings) were computed on the registered arms and stand as computed; this line changes nothing in them and adds the standing of the sideband sentence at this encoding: the margin over the one-token table survives the free previous token at equal dims on the pooled positions, with the coverage caveat; the finding's 'nothing reads this as context or as support for the sideband premise' stands; D_add is never read as context; no wall-clock, bytes or latency sentence follows (D-9; K-5 / K-6 stay UNTESTED; runtimes are elapsed time of the instrument process (time.time), not latency numbers). THREE BLOCKING objections, applied as one batch: a guard test that went red once the payload existed (fixed in the test, never in the instrument); the pinned B = 32 reading not said in words (now said, with B = 8); the COVERAGE CAVEAT above, which the SURVIVES texts had shipped without (now carried on every text with the hard bound, the critic's unregistered diagnostic named as such and re-run by the assistant with identical output, and the next rung corrected). SECOND PASS (the same day, a scoped text check of the batch): 659 numbers and 122 named-cell bindings checked, 0 misquotes; the three blockers confirmed closed; every 'context' / 'premise' occurrence a negation; ONE BLOCKING objection the batch had introduced, applied: the caveat's READING asserted a mechanism — an identity channel — that the diagnostic never measured, and misapplied it at unseen CURRENT tokens, where S[t] predates the token and can carry only the forward's prediction of it (free as p_t) or context; the READING now locates the margin without naming its mechanism, and the next rung gains a free arm from the stored p_t summaries. Nothing declined. Full outcome: the OUTCOME paragraph of the amendment block in docs/BLUEPRINT.md.

## F-27 — SEED-NOISE FLOOR (a methods datum; NON-CRITERION): on the four committed vanilla gamma-4 verdict runs a same-policy seed change moves pooled tokens/call by a CI95 half-width of 0.0385 / 0.0398 at 210 prompts and 0.0982 / 0.1032 at 30 (K3 / OQ15); one seed's per-prompt tokens/call correlates 0.4232 / 0.4172 with the other seed's; at n = 30 a +0.062 point win appears by seed noise alone in 0.1060 / 0.1320 of subsets and a single comparator leg of the F-19 WIN rule passes in 0.0235 / 0.0375 — FLOOR-MATERIAL on both populations, BAR-RESOLVED@210, RULE-LEAKS@30; a cross-seed, same-policy, single-comparator null
- date: 2026-10-04
- regime: REAL MODELS (a CPU recompute on committed evidence, no model forward, no GPU, no new seed): the per-prompt rows (n_emitted, n_blocks, n_drafted) of the four committed vanilla gamma-4 arm-1 summaries — Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct NF4 target + Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct bf16 draft, temperature 1.0, 256 new tokens, the HF adapter (acceptance-valid only, D-9); POPULATION K3 = data/prompts_k3_verdict.jsonl at seeds 0 and 1 (results/real/k3_verdict_arm1.summary.json, k3_verdict_s1_arm1.summary.json; F-19's seed pair, THE label population), POPULATION OQ15 = data/prompts_oq15_verdict.jsonl at seeds 2 and 3 (oq15c_verdict_arm1.summary.json, oq15c_verdict_s3_arm1.summary.json; the replication on different prompts and seeds); families instruct / story / summarize, 70 each, bound by the metas' prompt-file shas. Registered 2026-10-04 (docs/BLUEPRINT.md, "SEED-NOISE FLOOR READOUTS REGISTRATION", written before any readout of a seed pair ran, three critic rounds before the freeze), frozen at 906edfd; both runs from the clean tree at that commit. Estimand E6's: tokens/call = sum n_emitted / sum n_blocks on a prompt set; the null delta = the lower seed minus the higher on the same prompts; CI95 by sideband.stats.paired_ratio_delta_ci (n_boot 2000, seed 0), exactly as F-19 and F-21 compute a gate leg; 1,000 subsets without replacement per n below 210, the full set at 210. NON-CRITERION for every finding and kill status: a CROSS-SEED, same-policy, SINGLE-COMPARATOR null, while the record's gate legs are same-seed (D-6) and all-comparator; that same-seed null is not measured (the cross-seed noise is plausibly an upper bound on it, argued from the shared prefix for a policy whose per-prompt variance does not exceed vanilla's; not measured).
- source: results/real/noise_floor_k3.json, results/real/noise_floor_k3.json.state.jsonl (K3, the label population), results/real/noise_floor_oq15.json, results/real/noise_floor_oq15.json.state.jsonl (OQ15, the replication); each state file holds one row per (n, subset) with the subset's prompt indices and swap mask, and the header binds the four summary and meta shas and the prompt file's sha; inputs results/real/k3_verdict_arm1.summary.json, results/real/k3_verdict_s1_arm1.summary.json, results/real/oq15c_verdict_arm1.summary.json, results/real/oq15c_verdict_s3_arm1.summary.json with their .meta.json, data/prompts_k3_verdict.jsonl, data/prompts_oq15_verdict.jsonl; the F-19 val numbers from results/real/k3_threshold_search_arm4.json and results/real/k3_val_arm1.summary.json; instrument tools/run_noise_floor.py (attested by both payloads; frozen); landing test tests/test_noise_floor_landing.py; the registration block and its OUTCOME in docs/BLUEPRINT.md; the methods note docs/NOISE_FLOOR.md.

  (two-tower) D = Mt - Mn_r exactly, both sharing the fplus base: on the
  primary Mt straddling zero (point zero) and Mn_r straddling zero (point
  negative); on the replication Mt CI-clear positive and Mn_r CI-clear
  positive; Mt's point is at or above +0.005 on the replication (the triple
  rule fires there; under R-T an architecture comparison, fplus having no
  tower B): on the replication the twin's Mn_r +0.004847 covers 0.82 of Mt

  exactly, both sharing the fplus base: on the primary Mt CI-clear positive
  and Mn_r CI-clear positive; on the replication Mt CI-clear positive and

  so D sits near zero: the corrected twin matches the aligned block's gain

  same-marginal sparse block of these dims yields about Mt); WHAT THE

  +0.0388 [+0.0279, +0.0501] / +0.0380 [+0.0264, +0.0491]), so the readers
  read the free baseline better than R-0 did and the aligned block no

  m_SLOTS 124.0) / c_HZ 0.1740 (m_HZ 4097.2, m_SLOTS 124.0) before training
  (under AdamW, scaling one input family by c equals scaling its first-layer
  initialisation and effective learning rate by c); the two run payloads'
  `registered` field carries the frozen tool's own constant, the
  READER-AND-LENS block's name, as every payload of that tool does, while
  the registration of record for these runs is this block, bound by the
  launch HEAD, the s1 stage, the registered out names and the word payload's
  `registration` field; the S3 word K-2-ISOLATED (final) does not move.
- caveats: Every label is a statement about THAT reader at the S1 gate cell
  on one D row per harvest; the three together do not establish K-2's
  universal "never beat" (one more family of readers); R-T's licence
  readouts compare architectures; ABSORBED is the registered strong-null
  form (every D upper below +0.005 on both harvests), not a demonstration
  that the equal-dims margin is zero; each reader was built against one of
  F-24's two named mechanisms (R-N and R-T against the hz block's dominance
  of the input norm, by rescaling it and by giving the slots block its own
  tower; R-L against the one-to-three-pass budget), and a reader against
  both at once was not run; neither the rescale nor the longer budget moved
  the early VAL peak F-24 (iv) disclosed: the h_a arms' best VAL epochs
  stayed at most 2 of 30 under R-N and 2 of 60 under R-L, so the chosen R-L
  models are still one-to-three-pass readers, at a third of the learning
  rate; the gate rows' CI95 are prompt-cluster bootstraps over successes
  summed across the three train seeds, not seed-inclusive intervals, and a
  seed-inclusive approximation (unregistered; the critic's recompute round,
  the image audit's construction) keeps every D upper below +0.005 while
  marking some CI-clear Mt and Mn_r readings as seed-sensitive, so the
  shapes above are read at the registered intervals; the corrected twin's
  own cost or gain (Mn_r) is quoted beside every D; S1 L2 and L3 are not
  re-tested here (L3's lens is one mega-group); no reader-matched rider ran
  (owed only on a REOPENED word); the S3 word K-2-ISOLATED (final) does not
  move; the earlier block's conditional STAGE S1 stays unrun and its words
  stand as written.
